# ecomOS workflows

235 predetermined workflows. Each is how a good person in the role would do the job, step by step, with inputs, the skill each step uses, the output and the check before the next step. Generated by `scripts/build_workflows.py`.

## Orchestrator (ORCH), 1 workflows

### ORCH-W01 Onboard a brand through Door B

Trigger: event: new brand added with a website. Human role: account manager onboarding a client. Capabilities: ORCH-C08. Needs: website URL, founder contact, door and approval chain defaults.

1. **Read the site.** Run BRN-W01 steps 2 and 3 for the first time: full crawl, snapshot, claims tiered as site-backed, first speed run. Skill: `brn/site-diff`. Output: brand record v0, first speed measurement. Check: products, policies and claims each have a page source.
2. **Connect sources.** Present the connector list. For each granted source run the collector and compute the contract fields it enables. Skill: `none`. Output: connections with status, contract partial. Check: ungranted sources show as unavailable with a connect card. Ask the founder if: a source needs a grant only the founder can give.
3. **Ingest documents and notes.** Extract facts with sources from Drive, Notion and uploads. Raise conflicts with the site. Skill: `brn/document-extract`. Output: facts, conflicts. Check: a summary is not a fact.
4. **Interview rounds.** Ask what data cannot answer: margins, what was tried, competitors as the founder sees them, red lines, real policies. Store answers as decisions. Skill: `shared/founder-question-round`. Output: decisions log entries. Check: no question the brain can already answer. Ask the founder if: always; this step is the founder.
5. **First break and top 10.** Run DIAG-W01 and PROP-W01 on what is readable. Show the Today screen with unavailable stages named. Skill: `none`. Output: Today screen v1. Check: every unavailable stage names its connector.
6. **Team and rooms.** Invite people to rooms per the approval chain. Build queues. Skill: `orch/queue-build`. Output: roles, queues. Check: money, writes and red lines stay with the founder unless handed over in writing.

Outputs: brand brain v1, connections, Today screen, roles. QA: every fact has a source; no external write happened. Read point: 28 days: brain health and first reads. Duration: 1 day of agent time spread over the founder's answers.

## Diagnose (DIAG), 1 workflows

### DIAG-W01 Find the break

Trigger: scheduled daily after BRN-W01. Human role: business analyst running the morning numbers. Capabilities: DIAG-C01, DIAG-C02, DIAG-C03. Needs: contract values and 4 trailing windows, store tags, benchmarks by store type, founder goal for the period.

1. **Compute deltas and stake.** In code: current vs own trailing median vs peer band per pipeline stage, and money at stake per stage. Skill: `shared/metric-read`. Output: delta table. Check: every row has a window ending on a full day.
2. **Rank the breaks.** Sort stages by stake, tie-break by confidence then upstream position. Unreadable stages listed separately. Skill: `diag/break-ranking`. Output: ranked breaks, unreadable list. Check: an unreadable stage is never marked healthy.
3. **Write the proof.** For the top 3 breaks, the numbers, the window, what changed and since when. No fix words. Skill: `diag/break-proof`. Output: proof lines. Check: every number quoted is in the delta table. Ask the founder if: two breaks tie and the numbers cannot settle it.
4. **Founder line.** One sentence naming the break, one on why it is first, one on the next in line. Skill: `shared/plain-english-card`. Output: founder_line, next_break_line. Check: reading level under grade 10.

Outputs: breaks[], unreadable[], founder lines. QA: no fix language; windows on full days. Read point: none. Duration: 2 minutes.

## Propose (PROP), 1 workflows

### PROP-W01 Build the top 10 for a break

Trigger: scheduled daily after DIAG-W01, or on demand for a chosen break. Human role: head of growth prioritising the week. Capabilities: PROP-C01, PROP-C02, PROP-C03, PROP-C05, PROP-C06, PROP-C07. Needs: the break with proof, store tags, red lines, registry exclusions and priors, reach numbers for the stage.

1. **Audit the surface.** Dispatch the surface auditor for this break. Speed: the lab on 3 pages. Conversion: funnel pages and checkout. Retention: flows and order gaps. Paid: structure and results. Skill: `shared/surface-audit`. Output: observed conditions with evidence. Check: every condition has evidence a person can verify. Ask the founder if: the audit needs an access grant.
2. **Filter the bank.** In code: ideas by bottleneck and store tags, minus exclusions with a live reason. Skill: `none`. Output: candidate ideas. Check: no excluded idea present.
3. **Match signals.** Batches of 40: does each idea's signal fire on the observed conditions, with the quote. Skill: `prop/signal-match`. Output: fired ideas with evidence. Check: every fired idea quotes a condition.
4. **Web pass.** Verify time-sensitive claims and search for tactics the bank lacks, within the budget. New candidates to the candidates file. Skill: `shared/web-research-budgeted`. Output: facts and candidates with URL and date. Check: budget not exceeded; every fact dated.
5. **Fill evidence fields and rank.** Reach from the contract, effect basis from the record or source, brand prior from the registry, effort, cost, risk, dependencies, founder weight. Then the code sort. Skill: `shared/rank-candidates`. Output: ranked list with score parts. Check: source carries no weight.
6. **Challenge the top 15.** Re-check each against the audit. Demote weak signals and overstated effects with a one-line reason. Promote stronger audit or web ideas. Skill: `prop/challenge-idea`. Output: final order, demoted[]. Check: every change from the code sort is written down. Ask the founder if: two strong cards conflict.
7. **Write the cards.** Top 10 as plain-English cards with metric, read point, stop rule, owner specialist and workflow id from the routing table. Skill: `shared/plain-english-card`. Output: cards[]. Check: every card names an observed condition and a read point.

Outputs: cards[], demoted[], candidates file entries. QA: no card crosses a red line or a live exclusion; effect sizes from the record or a dated source only. Read point: per card, at its read point. Duration: 20 to 40 minutes, mostly sub-agents.

## Registrar (REG), 1 workflows

### REG-W01 Take a read

Trigger: event: a registry entry reaches its read point. Human role: analyst closing an experiment. Capabilities: REG-C02, REG-C03, REG-C04, REG-C06. Needs: the entry with metric, window, kill and scale rule, contract at read time.

1. **Read the metric.** Pull the metric for the entry's window. Early or unavailable is recorded as such, never as a result. Skill: `shared/metric-read`. Output: value with window. Check: window matches the rule.
2. **Compare to the rule.** In code: up, down, flat, or unreadable against the kill and scale rule. Skill: `none`. Output: result. Check: no result without a full window.
3. **Write the learning.** One plain sentence with the number. Update the brand's prior for the idea. If agency-run and not opted out, update the overlay with direction and tags only. Skill: `reg/learning-line`. Output: learning line, prior update, overlay row. Check: overlay carries no brand identity.
4. **Notify.** Tell the Orchestrator so the Today screen and the weekly review pick it up. Skill: `none`. Output: event. Check: None.

Outputs: read result, learning line, prior update. QA: every result quotes the window. Read point: this is the read. Duration: 1 minute.

## QA Gate (QA), 1 workflows

### QA-W01 Gate an artefact

Trigger: event: a card, artefact or report is ready. Human role: editor and compliance reviewer. Capabilities: QA-C01, LOOP-C12, LOOP-C12, QA-C07. Needs: the artefact, claims tiers, prices and policies, voice examples and forbidden words, contract fields cited, write-permission table, competitor text corpus.

1. **Claims.** Every factual sentence traced to an approved or site-backed claim. Forbidden fails. Skill: `shared/claim-check`. Output: claim verdicts. Check: every fact sentence has a verdict. Ask the founder if: an untiered claim would matter to a customer.
2. **Numbers.** Recompute every number from the contract or record in code. Windows on full days. Ratios from totals. Skill: `shared/metric-read`. Output: number verdicts. Check: no number outside the record.
3. **Writes, placeholders, competitor text, voice, reading level, red lines, completeness.** Run the remaining checks in order. Return every failing line with what is wrong and the fix. Skill: `qa/artefact-checks`. Output: pass or failures[]. Check: failures name the line.
4. **Skill audit.** Does the artefact match the skill it claims to have followed. Mismatches are warnings for the skill owner. Skill: `none`. Output: warnings[]. Check: None.

Outputs: pass or failures[], warnings[]. QA: the gate never edits; it returns. Read point: none. Duration: 2 to 5 minutes.

## Brand Brain Keeper (BRN), 1 workflows

### BRN-W01 Daily collection, crawl and contract

Trigger: scheduled daily 04:00. Human role: data analyst and site auditor. Capabilities: BRN-C01, BRN-C02, BRN-C03, BRN-C05, BRN-C06. Needs: connector list with access levels, last site snapshot, claims tiers.

1. **Run collectors.** Pull orders, sessions, spend, flows, tickets, reviews and competitor snapshots for yesterday. Stamp each pull. Skill: `none`. Output: warehouse rows with freshness. Check: every connector either landed rows or marked its fields unavailable. Ask the founder if: a connector lost access.
2. **Crawl and diff the site.** Crawl key pages by script. Diff products, prices, stock, policies, claims and scripts against the last snapshot. Skill: `brn/site-diff`. Output: change log entries. Check: every change has a page URL and a before and after value.
3. **Tier new claims.** Any claim seen for the first time is matched to the tiers. Unmatched claims become founder questions. Skill: `brn/claim-tiering`. Output: tiered claims, open questions. Check: no claim marked approved without a founder decision. Ask the founder if: 4 or more untiered claims.
4. **Detect conflicts.** Compare document facts, interview answers and site facts. Site wins by default; raise the rest. Skill: `brn/conflict-check`. Output: conflicts list. Check: each conflict quotes both sources with dates. Ask the founder if: a conflict blocks a running item.
5. **Compute the contract.** Recompute every metric contract field in code. Windows end on yesterday. Missing stays unavailable. Skill: `shared/metric-read`. Output: contract values with windows and freshness. Check: no field is 0 where inputs were missing.
6. **Brain health note.** Write freshness and confidence per section and the list of unavailable fields with the source that would fix each. Skill: `none`. Output: brain health JSON for the Today screen. Check: every unavailable field names a connector.

Outputs: warehouse rows, change log, open questions, contract values, brain health. QA: every fact has a source and a date; no section claims high confidence with an unavailable input. Read point: none; this is the data step. Duration: 15 minutes of scripts, no waiting.

## Site (SITE), 30 workflows

### SITE-W01 Choose the platform, stand up the account and secure the domain

Trigger: on demand. Human role: New store setup lead. Capabilities: SITE-C01, SITE-C02, SITE-C03. Needs: brand brain: budget and team capacity, brand brain: catalogue size and SKU complexity, brand brain: legal entity, founder decision log.

1. **List required features before comparing platforms.** Reads catalogue size, SKU complexity, budget and design needs and turns them into a written feature list before looking at any platform. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist`. Output: feature requirement list. Check: Every item on the list ties to a real product or catalogue fact, not a guess..
2. **Score total cost of ownership.** Scores hosting, apps, transaction fees and developer time as one true yearly cost per platform (Shopify, WooCommerce, custom), not the sticker price. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist`. Output: cost comparison table. Check: Every cost line carries a source and a date..
3. **Recommend platform and plan tier.** Picks the platform and plan tier with the lowest true cost, and names the exact trigger (order volume, feature need) that should force an upgrade. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist`. Output: platform recommendation memo. Check: The memo states a numeric upgrade trigger, not a vague 'when you grow'.. Ask the founder if: budget or catalogue size has not been confirmed with the founder.
4. **Register the account and enter legal identity.** Registers under an email the business permanently controls and enters the exact legal identity before any payment or tax setting is touched. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist`. Output: setup checklist entries. Check: Account email is a business domain address, not a personal inbox..
5. **Set defaults and turn on account security.** Sets currency, region and unit defaults once, before any price is entered, and turns on two-factor authentication before payments are activated. Skill: `none`. Output: security configuration report. Check: 2FA confirmed on before any gateway is touched, checked live not from a settings screenshot..
6. **Set staff roles and time-bound agency access.** Creates custom staff roles and gives any agency collaborator access that expires on a set date rather than standing forever. Skill: `none`. Output: security configuration report. Check: No collaborator has owner-level access without an end date.. Ask the founder if: an agency needs standing access beyond the current project.
7. **Buy the domain and avoid the platform default.** Filters candidate names by brandability, buys the real domain and never launches on the platform's default subdomain. Skill: `none`. Output: DNS change list. Check: The live storefront never resolves to a *.myshopify.com or platform default URL..
8. **Point DNS for the platform and for sender authentication.** Points DNS at the platform and reuses the same DNS workflow to add SPF, DKIM and DMARC records for sender domain authentication. Skill: `none`. Output: DNS change list. Check: Every DNS record is verified resolving live, not just saved in the registrar panel..
9. **Register near-miss domains and matching TLDs.** Registers the near-miss spellings and additional TLDs a squatter would use to imitate the brand. Skill: `none`. Output: domain readiness report. Check: Registrar choice favors portability over convenience unless there is a real reason not to.. Ask the founder if: budget is tight and squatting risk is genuinely low for this category.

Outputs: platform recommendation memo, cost comparison table, setup checklist, security configuration report, DNS change list, domain readiness report. QA: Every claim and number traces to the brand record or a dated web source.; 2FA is on before payments are touched.; No leftover agency access without an end date.; DNS verified live, not just saved.. Read point: Kevin reviews the memo and checklist before any payment is set live; DNS re-checked 24-48h after propagation.. Duration: agent: 2-3 hours across the three capabilities; waiting: up to 48 hours for DNS propagation and domain registration confirmation..

### SITE-W02 Select the theme and put safe engineering practice behind it

Trigger: on demand | store setup, or after a near-miss where a live edit could not be traced or reverted. Human role: Theme developer / engineering lead. Capabilities: SITE-C04, SITE-C05, SITE-C62, SITE-C69, SITE-C70. Needs: brand brain: required PDP/collection features, founder decision log, GitHub repo access or Shopify CLI.

1. **List required sections and features.** Writes the list of sections and features a theme must have before browsing any theme store. Skill: `site/theme-engineering`. Output: feature requirement list. Check: List is written before a single theme is opened..
2. **Vet 2-3 candidate themes.** Vets each candidate on PDP quality, mobile behaviour and code quality, not the homepage demo, and scores each against the feature list. Skill: `site/theme-engineering`. Output: theme shortlist with scores. Check: Every theme is checked on a real PDP and a real mobile width, not the marketplace preview..
3. **Decide free vs paid and pick one.** Picks the theme with the best fit-to-cost, not the best looking demo. Skill: `none`. Output: theme decision memo. Check: Decision states the runner-up and why it lost.. Ask the founder if: two themes score within 5% and cost differs materially.
4. **Duplicate the theme before any edit round.** Never edits the live theme directly; every change round starts on a duplicate or a branch. Skill: `site/theme-pr-diff`. Output: duplicate theme / branch. Check: The live theme's edit history shows zero direct edits..
5. **Stand up git, CI and a staging theme.** Connects the theme repo to GitHub, adds Shopify CLI for local development, runs theme-check linting on every commit, and builds a staging theme URL that auto-deploys from a branch. Skill: `site/theme-engineering/git-ci-pipeline`. Output: git repo with commit history, CI config, staging theme URL. Check: A commit with a hard theme-check error is blocked from merging..
6. **Write the AI build brief before any AI agent touches the storefront.** Gives the build agent real brand inputs (products, policy, voice), lets it propose a plan first, and asks for exactly one storefront change per prompt. Skill: `site/theme-engineering/ai-build-guardrail`. Output: AI build brief. Check: The brief is written and approved before the agent makes its first live edit..
7. **Preview privately and QA every AI-authored section.** Previews every AI-built section privately, never on the public URL, and treats the output as a draft needing manual QA and a claims check. Skill: `shared/claim-check`. Output: QA pass report. Check: No AI-authored copy ships without a claim-check pass..
8. **Require review before merge and keep a tested rollback.** Requires a second reviewer or the founder gate before merging staging to the live theme, and keeps a one-command rollback to the previous release. Skill: `site/theme-engineering/git-ci-pipeline`. Output: change log, rollback runbook. Check: Rollback has actually been run once in a drill, not just documented..
9. **Document design tokens and check new sections against them.** Documents color, spacing, typography and component tokens from the live theme, then checks every new section against them and flags drift. Skill: `none`. Output: design token doc, drift flags. Check: A new section that doesn't match a token is flagged before it ships, not after..
10. **Maintain a staging environment with real-looking data.** Keeps a staging copy of the store, theme and a recent product/price snapshot in sync with production, refreshed on a schedule, for every risky change or app trial. Skill: `none`. Output: staging store, refresh schedule. Check: Every app trial and AI build-agent session runs against staging, never production..

Outputs: theme shortlist with scores, git repo with commit history + CI config, AI build brief, QA pass report, design token doc, staging store + refresh schedule, rollback runbook. QA: No edit ever lands on the live theme without a duplicate/branch and a reviewed PR.; Rollback command tested, not assumed.; AI-authored copy passed claim-check before merge.; New sections checked against the design token doc.. Read point: Founder reviews the PR before merge; token drift checked at every new section's QA.. Duration: agent: 1-2 days for initial engineering setup; ongoing: 30-60 minutes per theme change afterward..

### SITE-W03 Build the product catalogue and site architecture

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Catalogue and information-architecture builder. Capabilities: SITE-C06, SITE-C07. Needs: brand brain: products (prices, weights, stock, images), brand brain: audience, connector: shopify admin | woocommerce admin.

1. **Match product type to real fulfilment.** Sets each product's type to match how it is actually sold and fulfilled, since this drives tax, shipping and reporting behaviour. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist`. Output: catalogue completeness report. Check: Every product type matches a real fulfilment path, not a default guess..
2. **Turn on inventory tracking and assign SKUs.** Turns on inventory tracking and gives every product a real SKU. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist`. Output: field-by-field fix list. Check: No product ships without a SKU..
3. **Enter cost per item.** Enters a real cost per item on every product so margin reporting works from day one. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist`. Output: field-by-field fix list. Check: Cost field is populated with a real number, not left at zero or the platform default..
4. **Build variants and verify swatches.** Builds variant options, prices every row, and verifies swatch colors against real hex values. Skill: `none`. Output: field-by-field fix list. Check: Every variant row has a price; every swatch matches the real product color..
5. **Group gallery images by variant.** Groups product images by variant so the gallery updates correctly when a shopper changes a selection. Skill: `none`. Output: catalogue completeness report. Check: No product ships with 0 or 1 image..
6. **Decide manual vs smart collections.** Decides collections deliberately, manual for curated sets and smart (rule-based) for anything that should update itself, and deletes unused default demo collections. Skill: `site/page-spec-and-copy/homepage-collection-ux`. Output: navigation and collection spec. Check: No leftover demo collection remains live..
7. **Set tag discipline.** Builds a tag taxonomy so every filter a shopper can select actually returns matching products. Skill: `site/page-spec-and-copy/homepage-collection-ux`. Output: tag taxonomy. Check: Every filter tested live returns at least one correct result..
8. **Build navigation around how shoppers think.** Builds the menu from the shopper's own mental model, capped at 7 top-level items, reachable in 2-3 clicks. Skill: `none`. Output: navigation and collection spec. Check: A fresh pair of eyes reaches any product in 2-3 clicks.. Ask the founder if: the catalogue does not cleanly fit into 7 or fewer top categories.
9. **Turn on and QA breadcrumbs.** Turns on breadcrumb navigation across PDP and collection pages and verifies it live. Skill: `none`. Output: navigation and collection spec. Check: Breadcrumbs are correct on every template, checked live not in the editor..

Outputs: catalogue completeness report, field-by-field fix list, navigation and collection spec, tag taxonomy. QA: Every product has a SKU, a real cost, and at least 2 images.; Every filter tag actually returns matching products when clicked live.; Navigation tested in 2-3 clicks by someone unfamiliar with the site.. Read point: Checked again at pre-launch QA (SITE-W08) and whenever the catalogue changes materially.. Duration: agent: 4-8 hours depending on catalogue size; human: spot-checks on the live storefront..

### SITE-W04 Draft legal pages and configure the checkout base

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Store compliance and checkout configuration lead. Capabilities: SITE-C08, SITE-C18. Needs: brand brain: shipping and returns practice, brand brain: policies, connector: shopify admin.

1. **Draft policy pages from real practice.** Drafts shipping, returns, privacy and terms pages from the brand's real practice, never a bare template with placeholders left in. Skill: `site/page-spec-and-copy`. Output: draft policy pages. Check: No placeholder text remains anywhere in a policy page..
2. **Write real shipping terms with a duty disclosure.** Writes shipping terms that match reality, including a cross-border duties disclosure for any market where it applies. Skill: `none`. Output: draft policy pages. Check: Duty disclosure present for every cross-border market the brand actually ships to..
3. **Route to a human for legal sign-off.** Sends every AI-drafted policy page to a person for legal sign-off before it publishes. Skill: `shared/claim-check`. Output: legal review request. Check: No policy page auto-publishes without a human sign-off.. Ask the founder if: no lawyer is available to sign off.
4. **Point checkout's agree-box at the real terms page.** Wires the checkout's terms checkbox to the actual published terms page, not a draft or an old version. Skill: `none`. Output: checkout configuration. Check: The link in the live checkout resolves to the current, signed-off terms page..
5. **Keep guest checkout on.** Confirms guest checkout is on and stays on; account creation is never forced. Skill: `none`. Output: checkout configuration. Check: A test purchase completes without creating an account..
6. **Minimize checkout fields and separate consent.** Sets checkout fields to the minimum needed and separates transactional consent from marketing consent so a shopper is never opted into marketing by finishing a purchase. Skill: `none`. Output: checkout configuration. Check: Marketing consent checkbox is unchecked by default..
7. **Brand the checkout.** Applies the brand's logo and colors to checkout within the plan's real branding limits. Skill: `none`. Output: checkout configuration. Check: Checkout visually matches the brand, within what the plan tier allows..
8. **Cap coupons and order quantity.** Enables coupons with a per-user limit and a maximum spend cap, and sets a per-order quantity cap when stock is scarce. Skill: `none`. Output: settings change list. Check: A coupon cannot be reused past its cap in a live test..
9. **Set one abandoned-checkout email owner.** Turns on exactly one system (site platform or lifecycle platform, not both) to own the abandoned-checkout email. Skill: `none`. Output: settings change list. Check: A real abandoned cart triggers exactly one recovery email, confirmed by test..

Outputs: draft policy pages, legal review request, checkout configuration, settings change list. QA: No placeholder text left in a policy page.; Guest checkout confirmed on, tested live.; Exactly one system owns the abandoned-checkout email.. Read point: Before launch (feeds SITE-W08); re-checked whenever shipping or return practice changes.. Duration: agent: 3-5 hours; human: lawyer sign-off turnaround varies, days to weeks..

### SITE-W05 Configure payments, shipping and tax

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Finance operations setup lead. Capabilities: SITE-C09, SITE-C10, SITE-C11. Needs: brand brain: markets and currencies, brand brain: margin and AOV, brand brain: fulfilment locations, brand brain: registered tax jurisdictions, a real tax professional's confirmation of obligations.

1. **Verify identity documents for gateway activation.** Verifies the exact identity documents each payment gateway needs before activation, since a mismatch delays go-live by days. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist/payments-shipping-tax`. Output: payment setup checklist. Check: Documents match the gateway's stated requirements exactly..
2. **Add a second gateway and regional methods.** Adds a primary and a second payment gateway plus the regional payment methods that matter in each key market. Skill: `none`. Output: payment setup checklist. Check: Every key market has at least one locally trusted payment method.. Ask the founder if: a market's dominant local payment method is not yet supported.
3. **Set capture timing and fraud screening.** Sets capture timing and turns on fraud screening, including a screen for fake COD orders where COD is offered. Skill: `none`. Output: payment setup checklist. Check: Fraud screening confirmed active with a test transaction..
4. **Run the sandbox-then-real go-live test.** Tests every gateway in sandbox, then places one real order and refunds it before the storefront password comes off. Skill: `none`. Output: go-live test log. Check: A real refunded order appears in the gateway dashboard, not assumed from settings alone..
5. **Build shipping zones and assign fulfilment locations.** Gives every target country an explicit shipping zone and rate, and assigns a fulfilment location to each zone. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist/payments-shipping-tax`. Output: shipping configuration. Check: No country is left in the platform's undefined 'rest of world' bucket by accident..
6. **Set the free-shipping threshold from real numbers.** Sets the free-shipping threshold from the brand's real margin and AOV, and shows the threshold live in cart. Skill: `none`. Output: shipping configuration. Check: Threshold is computed from real margin data, not copied from a competitor..
7. **Test every rate bracket edge.** Tests carts at every weight or value bracket boundary before launch, since edge cases are where shipping math breaks. Skill: `none`. Output: rate QA log. Check: Every bracket boundary tested with a real cart, not just the middle of each bracket..
8. **Confirm real tax obligations before configuring.** Confirms the brand's real tax obligations with a professional before enabling any calculation, and turns on the platform tax service per registered region. Skill: `none`. Output: tax configuration. Check: No jurisdiction is configured without a professional's confirmation on file.. Ask the founder if: no tax professional has confirmed obligations yet.
9. **Decide display and basis rules per jurisdiction.** Decides inclusive vs exclusive tax display once, consistently, and sets the tax basis (origin/destination) and shipping taxability per jurisdiction's real rule. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist/payments-shipping-tax`. Output: tax configuration. Check: Rules match each jurisdiction's actual law, not a single global default..
10. **Test one product per tax class in 2+ regions.** Tests one product per tax class in at least two regions before trusting the setup. Skill: `none`. Output: tax QA log. Check: Every tax class produces the expected tax amount in a live cart test in 2 regions..

Outputs: payment setup checklist, go-live test log, shipping configuration, rate QA log, tax configuration, tax QA log. QA: A real refunded order proves payment end to end.; Every shipping rate bracket tested at its edge.; Tax obligations confirmed by a professional, not assumed.; Statement descriptor matches the real brand name.. Read point: Immediately before launch; re-run tax QA whenever catalogue, zones or nexus changes.. Duration: agent: about 1 day of configuration; human: a few days waiting on gateway verification and tax sign-off..

### SITE-W06 Set up multi-market localization and consent-based tag gating

Trigger: on demand | store setup for an India or EU-facing brand, or a compliance audit finding. Human role: Global setup and compliance lead. Capabilities: SITE-C12, SITE-C78. Needs: brand brain: target markets, founder decision log, current tag/tracking-script list.

1. **Group countries by tax treatment.** Groups target countries into markets by shared tax treatment, not by geography. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist`. Output: market plan. Check: Every market group shares one real tax treatment, verified against SITE-W05's tax setup..
2. **Verify the plan tier supports the plan.** Checks the plan tier actually supports per-market theme customization before promising it to the founder. Skill: `none`. Output: market plan. Check: No market feature is promised that the current plan tier cannot deliver.. Ask the founder if: the plan does not support the localization the founder wants.
3. **Add languages with human review.** Adds languages with auto-translate plus a human review pass, never machine translation shipped unchecked. Skill: `none`. Output: localization configuration. Check: Every translated page has had at least one human review pass..
4. **Build a per-market catalogue.** Builds a catalogue per market, excluding products that cannot ship or are not compliant there. Skill: `none`. Output: localization configuration. Check: No non-compliant or unshippable product is visible in a market's catalogue..
5. **Round converted prices naturally.** Rounds converted per-market prices to a natural local ending instead of a raw currency conversion. Skill: `none`. Output: localization configuration. Check: Prices read naturally in the local currency, not as an odd conversion artifact..
6. **Inventory every firing tag and script.** Inventories every tracking pixel, tag and script currently firing on first page load before gating any of them. Skill: `none`. Output: gated tag list. Check: The inventory is built from a real network trace, not the tag manager's own list..
7. **Route tags behind consent, keyed to region.** Routes each tracking tag behind a consent-management layer keyed to the visitor's region and choice, with the correct default: opt-in for GDPR regions, DPDP's stricter default for India. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist`. Output: configured consent layer. Check: Default state matches the visitor's region's actual law..
8. **Confirm rejection actually blocks the script.** Confirms a rejected-consent visitor genuinely does not fire the gated scripts, checked in network requests, not the banner's own claim. Skill: `none`. Output: consent log. Check: A live network trace after rejection shows zero gated requests fired..
9. **Keep essential scripts ungated.** Keeps cart, checkout and payment scripts ungated so a consent choice never breaks a core function, and logs every consent choice for audit. Skill: `none`. Output: consent log. Check: Cart and checkout function identically regardless of the visitor's consent choice..

Outputs: market plan, localization configuration, configured consent layer, gated tag list, consent log. QA: Consent rejection actually blocks the network request, verified live.; Default consent state matches the visitor's region's law.; Cart/checkout scripts never blocked by a consent choice.. Read point: Founder reviews the market plan before go-live in a new country; consent audit re-run after any tag change.. Duration: agent: 1-2 days; human: translation review and legal confirmation, days..

### SITE-W07 Govern the platform: apps, integrations, automations, releases and the mobile app

Trigger: monthly | a manual double-entry process is found | a repetitive manual task is found | a platform release lands | per app release. Human role: Platform engineering lead. Capabilities: SITE-C13, SITE-C63, SITE-C71, SITE-C68, SITE-G03. Needs: connector: shopify admin apps list, brand brain: app inventory, external system's API docs when building an integration.

1. **Vet any new app before install.** Vets a candidate app against ratings and a real before/after speed test, and consolidates apps doing the same job. Skill: `site/platform-integrations`. Output: app audit report. Check: No app installed without a before/after speed measurement..
2. **Remove residual code after uninstall.** Searches for and removes residual theme code left behind after every app uninstall. Skill: `none`. Output: install/removal recommendation list. Check: A search of the theme code shows zero references to an uninstalled app..
3. **Map the integration's data contract.** Maps every field that must sync both ways (inventory, order status, tracking, loyalty points) before writing any integration code. Skill: `site/platform-integrations/custom-integration-build`. Output: data contract doc. Check: The data contract is written and reviewed before the first line of integration code..
4. **Build the integration or Flow automation.** Builds the custom app or platform Flow automation and registers the needed webhooks, storing API keys only in the platform's secure settings. Skill: `site/platform-integrations/custom-integration-build`. Output: working integration or Flow. Check: No API key appears in theme code, a prompt or a chat log..
5. **Add retry logic and a sync-gap alert.** Adds retry-with-backoff on failed webhook deliveries and alerts a human the moment a sync gap is detected, instead of failing silently. Skill: `none`. Output: webhook retry log. Check: A deliberately failed test webhook triggers a human alert within the target window..
6. **Wire no-code automation rules.** Lists recurring manual if-this-then-that actions and wires each into the platform's native automation layer instead of custom code. Skill: `site/platform-integrations`. Output: working automation rules. Check: Each rule tested against a real historical order before going live..
7. **Log every automated action.** Logs every action an automation rule takes so a human can audit what fired and why, and avoids stacking rules that touch the same object. Skill: `none`. Output: action audit log. Check: Every fired rule is traceable to a logged reason..
8. **Track platform releases on schedule.** Reads each Shopify Editions release, WooCommerce/WordPress core update, app deprecation notice and API sunset date on its own schedule. Skill: `site/platform-integrations`. Output: adopt-or-ignore memo per release. Check: Every release relevant to the brand's stack gets a memo within a week of landing..
9. **Flag app-retirement opportunities and breaking changes.** Matches new native features against the current paid app list to flag one worth retiring, and flags any breaking change against code the store actually uses. Skill: `none`. Output: deprecation deadline calendar. Check: Every API the store depends on has a tracked sunset date..
10. **QA a mobile app release, if the brand has one.** Checks the app store listing, screenshots, deep links from ads and email, the push opt-in prompt and app-only offers before each release. Skill: `site/platform-integrations`. Output: app release QA report. Check: Deep links tested from a real ad and a real email, not assumed.. Ask the founder if: the brand does not have or is not launching a mobile app.
11. **Report the adopt-or-ignore call.** Writes a short adopt-or-ignore recommendation per release instead of a full changelog dump. Skill: `none`. Output: adopt-or-ignore memo. Check: The memo is short enough to read in under 2 minutes..

Outputs: app audit report, working integration or Flow, webhook retry log, working automation rules + audit log, adopt-or-ignore memo, deprecation deadline calendar, app release QA report. QA: No API key ever appears in theme code, a prompt or a chat log.; Every automation rule tested against a real order before going live.; Sync gaps alert a human; nothing fails silently.. Read point: Monthly app/script review; immediately when a release or deprecation notice lands.. Duration: agent: 3-5 hours monthly; integration builds: 1-3 days depending on the external system..

### SITE-W08 Run pre-launch QA and launch sequencing

Trigger: event: pre-launch. Human role: Launch manager. Capabilities: SITE-C14. Needs: brand brain: full setup record, founder decision log, results from SITE-W01 through SITE-W07.

1. **Assemble the dependency-ordered checklist.** Pulls every prior setup workflow's result into one dependency-ordered launch checklist. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist/launch-sequencing`. Output: launch checklist skeleton. Check: Every prior setup workflow's output is represented as a checklist line..
2. **Buy something end to end.** Verifies every product is buyable end to end: browse, add to cart, checkout, pay, confirm. Skill: `none`. Output: launch checklist with pass/fail. Check: A real test purchase completes on at least one product per category..
3. **Confirm emails actually arrive.** Confirms confirmation and shipping emails arrive in a real inbox, not just show as 'configured' in settings. Skill: `none`. Output: launch checklist with pass/fail. Check: A real email is seen landing in an inbox, not assumed from a settings toggle..
4. **Run a full mobile QA pass.** Runs a full mobile QA pass on the live, password-protected storefront across the brand's real device split. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist/launch-sequencing`. Output: launch checklist with pass/fail. Check: Tested on the brand's actual top 2-3 devices, not a generic phone..
5. **Verify the logged-out storefront in incognito.** Opens an incognito window and clicks every visible element once to catch anything gated behind a stale session. Skill: `none`. Output: launch checklist with pass/fail. Check: Every visible element was clicked at least once..
6. **Close every step with a real customer action.** Closes every checklist step with the action a real customer would take, never a settings-page glance. Skill: `none`. Output: launch checklist with pass/fail. Check: No checklist line is marked pass from a settings screen alone..
7. **Compile the pass/fail table and go/no-go call.** Compiles the full pass/fail table and gives a clear go or no-go recommendation. Skill: `none`. Output: go/no-go recommendation. Check: The recommendation is a single clear call, not a hedge.. Ask the founder if: any step fails and the fix needs more than a small brief.
8. **Remove the password only at 100% pass.** Removes the storefront password only once every checklist step passes. Skill: `none`. Output: go/no-go recommendation. Check: Password removal timestamp is logged against the final checklist pass..
9. **Schedule the first hygiene sweep.** Schedules the first post-launch hygiene sweep for the end of week one. Skill: `none`. Output: go/no-go recommendation. Check: SITE-W09's first run is scheduled before this workflow closes..

Outputs: launch checklist with pass/fail, go/no-go recommendation. QA: Every checklist item closed with a real customer action, not an admin-panel check.; No password removed until 100% pass.. Read point: The go/no-go call, made once, right before removing the password.. Duration: agent: 4-6 hours; human: final go/no-go decision, same day..

### SITE-W09 Monitor post-launch hygiene, uptime and automated regression

Trigger: weekly | every theme or app deploy, and nightly | store setup completion. Human role: Site reliability and hygiene monitor. Capabilities: SITE-C15, SITE-C64, SITE-C65. Needs: connector: shopify admin, connector: support desk, staging theme URL, store URLs by page type.

1. **Reconcile inventory weekly in month one.** Reconciles platform inventory against real stock weekly for the first month after launch. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist/post-launch-hygiene`. Output: hygiene report. Check: Any mismatch is logged with the real stock count next to the platform count..
2. **Classify support tickets by root cause.** Classifies support tickets weekly by root cause to find a thin page or an unclear policy, and hands off anything outside Site's 4 folders. Skill: `site/handoff-note`. Output: fix list. Check: Every ticket cluster of 3+ maps to a named root cause or is handed off..
3. **Re-run shipping and tax checks on change.** Re-runs shipping and tax checks whenever a product, zone or app changes, instead of waiting for the next scheduled sweep. Skill: `none`. Output: hygiene report. Check: A changed zone or product triggers a same-week re-check..
4. **Confirm automations are actually sending.** Confirms review-request automation is actually sending on a real order, and the payout bank account matches the region's deposit type. Skill: `none`. Output: hygiene report. Check: A real recent order's timeline shows the review request fired..
5. **Sweep for broken links and redirect chains.** Sweeps for broken links to images, CSS and JS, and collapses any redirect chain to a single 301. Skill: `site/store-setup-checklist/post-launch-hygiene`. Output: fix list. Check: No redirect chain longer than one hop remains..
6. **Map critical paths into test cases.** Maps the store's critical paths (home, PDP, filter, cart, discount code, checkout, login) into automated test cases. Skill: `none`. Output: test coverage map. Check: Every critical path has at least one scripted test..
7. **Run the regression suite on every deploy and nightly.** Runs the suite before and after every theme or app deploy and nightly, with visual diffs across breakpoints and self-healing for cosmetic selector changes. Skill: `site/reliability-monitoring`. Output: pass/fail report, visual diff set. Check: A deploy never ships without a green regression run..
8. **Write a bug report with replay on failure.** Captures video replay, console errors and a network trace on any failure and writes a bug report a developer can act on without re-running it. Skill: `none`. Output: bug reports with replay. Check: The bug report is actionable without the developer re-running the test..
9. **Run synthetic uptime checks.** Runs synthetic checks every few minutes on home, PDP, cart, checkout and search from more than one region, plus a full transaction walk on a schedule. Skill: `site/reliability-monitoring`. Output: uptime dashboard. Check: A full add-to-cart-to-confirmation walk runs on its own schedule, not just a page-200 check..
10. **Track SSL, DNS and real-user load.** Tracks SSL certificate expiry and DNS resolution separately from page uptime, and pulls real-user page-load data to catch slow-but-not-down degradation. Skill: `none`. Output: uptime dashboard. Check: SSL expiry has its own alert, separate from the uptime check..
11. **Route alerts with escalation.** Routes any failure or degradation to a human with an escalation path if unacknowledged, and logs time-to-detect and time-to-fix. Skill: `none`. Output: incident log. Check: Every incident has a logged detect time and fix time..

Outputs: hygiene report, fix list, pass/fail regression report, bug reports with replay, uptime dashboard, incident log. QA: Every fail has a concrete fix, not a vague warning.; A theme/app deploy never ships without a green regression run.; An outage or degradation reaches a human within the alert routing's target time.. Read point: Weekly hygiene report; regression on every deploy and nightly; uptime continuous.. Duration: agent: continuous/automated; human: reviews the weekly report, 15-30 minutes..

### SITE-W10 Build repeat-purchase, wholesale and demand-capture features

Trigger: on demand | a high return or exchange volume is found | a top SKU stocks out or a drop is planned. Human role: Feature builder for subscriptions, loyalty, B2B, returns and waitlists. Capabilities: SITE-C16, SITE-C72, SITE-C79. Needs: brand brain: repeat purchase rate, brand brain: wholesale demand, margin data, returns app options.

1. **Check product-market fit for repeat purchase.** Checks the product and margin genuinely fit repeat purchase or bundling before proposing a subscription. Skill: `site/feature-build`. Output: feature setup spec. Check: Repeat-purchase or bundle fit is backed by a real repurchase-rate number.. Ask the founder if: repeat-purchase data is thin or unavailable.
2. **Add loyalty and wishlist deliberately.** Adds loyalty only when repeat purchase genuinely matters to margin, and adds a wishlist regardless since it costs little. Skill: `none`. Output: feature setup spec. Check: Loyalty is justified with a margin number, not added by default..
3. **Build a separate B2B path.** Builds a distinct B2B path with a price list and a minimum-order or net-terms rule where wholesale demand is real. Skill: `none`. Output: flow diagram. Check: B2B pricing never leaks into the consumer-facing price..
4. **Never pre-select a subscription option.** Never pre-selects a subscription option in a quiz or at checkout, and builds distinct pause, skip, swap and cancel flows. Skill: `none`. Output: flow diagram. Check: A subscription checkbox defaults to unchecked in a live test..
5. **Configure the returns portal against real policy.** Selects and configures a returns app or native flow against the brand's real return window and condition rules. Skill: `site/feature-build`. Output: configured returns portal. Check: Portal rules match the published return window exactly..
6. **Set a real exchange-for-credit incentive.** Sets an exchange-for-store-credit incentive sized against real margin, with copy matched word-for-word to the published returns policy. Skill: `none`. Output: exchange-credit rule. Check: Incentive amount is computed from real margin, not a round guess..
7. **Capture structured return reasons.** Surfaces return reasons in structured form so the data feeds product and copy fixes, and hands restocking/refund-timing rules to operations. Skill: `none`. Output: structured return-reason data feed. Check: Return reasons are queryable, not free text only..
8. **Build the restock notify-me capture.** Adds a notify-me capture on any out-of-stock PDP, firing an automatic notification the moment the SKU restocks, ranked by signup order. Skill: `site/feature-build`. Output: notify-me widget. Check: A test signup receives the restock notification automatically..
9. **Build a password-protected coming-soon page.** Builds a password-protected coming-soon page for a pre-launch or drop, kept out of search indexes. Skill: `none`. Output: coming-soon page. Check: The page is confirmed excluded from search indexing..
10. **Own the signup list and report conversion.** Captures every signup into the brand's own list, never a third party's, and reports waitlist size and back-in-stock conversion per SKU. Skill: `none`. Output: waitlist size and conversion report. Check: Signups land in the brand's own list, verified by export..

Outputs: feature setup spec, flow diagram, configured returns portal, exchange-credit rule, notify-me widget, coming-soon page, waitlist size and conversion report. QA: No subscription option pre-selected anywhere.; Returns portal copy matches the published policy word for word.; Waitlist signups land only in the brand's own list.. Read point: Founder gate before any feature goes live; waitlist conversion reviewed at the next reorder decision.. Duration: agent: 1-2 days per feature; human: app configuration and legal wording check..

### SITE-W12 Capture a speed baseline and fix Core Web Vitals

Trigger: monthly | on demand. Human role: Speed engineer, diagnosis. Capabilities: SITE-C21, SITE-C25, SITE-C26, SITE-C27. Needs: brand brain: top paid landing page and PDP URLs, connector: pagespeed insights api.

1. **Record the real page before touching anything.** Records every response of the real ad-landing page and top PDP with speed_lab.record before changing a single byte. Skill: `site/speed-audit`. Output: recorded page capture. Check: Recording is fresh, taken today, not reused from last month..
2. **Measure 3 runs on a throttled mobile profile.** Replays and measures 3 runs on a Pixel 5 profile, slow 4G, 4x CPU, and takes the median. Skill: `site/speed-audit`. Output: baseline measurement (results/*.json). Check: 3 runs recorded and averaged, never 1..
3. **Cross-check against field data.** Cross-checks a suspicious lab result against PageSpeed Insights field data before trusting it. Skill: `none`. Output: baseline measurement. Check: Lab and field numbers are compared, not just the lab number alone..
4. **Confirm the fix the page paid ads land on first.** Confirms the measurement actually saw the latest change, and fixes the page paid ads land on before any other page. Skill: `none`. Output: root-cause diagnosis. Check: The paid landing page is the first template diagnosed, not the homepage..
5. **Diagnose the exact CLS element.** Names the exact element causing layout shift per template and reserves space for anything arriving after first paint. Skill: `site/speed-audit/core-web-vitals-fix`. Output: CLS diagnosis per template. Check: The shifting element is named, not described as 'the page feels jumpy'..
6. **Fix conflicting CSS and remove fade-ins.** Fixes contradicting CSS rules, moves late inline styles into the head, and removes fade-in entrance effects on in-view elements. Skill: `none`. Output: CLS diagnosis per template. Check: CLS re-measured after the fix, not assumed fixed..
7. **Name the LCP element and run the fix order.** Names the LCP element per template and runs the fix order: un-lazy, de-animate, compress, preload, then cut the blocking script hiding it. Skill: `site/speed-audit/core-web-vitals-fix`. Output: LCP diagnosis and fix sequence. Check: Fix order followed in sequence, not skipped to preloading first..
8. **Check TTFB before blaming the image.** Checks time-to-first-byte before blaming the image, and preloads only the true LCP image with high fetch priority. Skill: `none`. Output: LCP diagnosis and fix sequence. Check: TTFB checked and ruled out or fixed before an image fix is proposed..
9. **Cut main-thread JS blocking key interactions.** Cuts main-thread JavaScript blocking add-to-cart, variant pickers, filters and search autocomplete. Skill: `site/speed-audit/core-web-vitals-fix`. Output: INP diagnosis. Check: Autocomplete lag treated as a real INP issue, not ignored..
10. **Confirm with a real tap test and fidelity check.** Confirms every fix with a real tap test on Add to Cart, not just a lab score, and runs check_fidelity before trusting a variant. Skill: `none`. Output: root-cause diagnosis. Check: Fidelity check passed before any number is trusted..

Outputs: baseline measurement (results/*.json), root-cause diagnosis, CLS diagnosis per template, LCP diagnosis and fix sequence, INP diagnosis. QA: 3 runs averaged, never 1.; Fidelity check passed before any number is trusted.; A real tap test confirms the INP fix, not the score alone.. Read point: Immediately after each fix, and again after about 28 days of field data.. Duration: agent: 3-5 hours; measurement runs: minutes each, parallelizable..

### SITE-W13 Cut image, font and code weight

Trigger: monthly | on demand. Human role: Speed engineer, asset optimization. Capabilities: SITE-C22, SITE-C24, SITE-C30. Needs: connector: shopify admin | woocommerce media library, brand brain: brand fonts, results/*.json history.

1. **Check images directly.** Checks images directly rather than trusting automatic platform compression alone. Skill: `site/speed-audit/asset-weight`. Output: image fix list with before/after sizes. Check: Before/after byte counts shown for every flagged image..
2. **Resize and pick the right format.** Resizes to about 2,000px before upload, uses JPEG for photos and PNG only for transparency, never GIF. Skill: `none`. Output: image fix list. Check: No GIF remains in the product media library..
3. **Confirm modern format delivery and lazy-load rules.** Confirms the platform/CDN delivers WebP or AVIF, and lazy-loads below the fold while eager-loading the hero and first-card images. Skill: `none`. Output: image fix list. Check: Hero image confirmed eager-loaded, below-fold images confirmed lazy..
4. **Reserve space and sweep stray heavy images.** Adds width/height or aspect-ratio to every image to prevent shift, and sweeps the media library for stray multi-MB non-product images. Skill: `none`. Output: image fix list. Check: No image over the brand's size ceiling remains in the live media library..
5. **Cap font variations and self-host.** Keeps to about 3 font variations and self-hosts fonts as WOFF2 instead of calling Google Fonts. Skill: `none`. Output: font fix list. Check: Font requests confirmed served from the brand's own domain..
6. **Add font-display: swap and match fallback metrics.** Adds font-display: swap to every @font-face rule and styles the fallback font to match size and line height. Skill: `none`. Output: font fix list. Check: No visible text jump on font swap, confirmed with a filmstrip..
7. **Subset fonts on a framework storefront.** Subsets fonts to the characters actually used on a headless or framework storefront. Skill: `none`. Output: font fix list. Check: Font file size reduced and confirmed with a before/after byte count..
8. **Minify and split code by page type.** Minifies and splits CSS/JS by page type, only after images and scripts are already fixed. Skill: `site/speed-audit/asset-weight`. Output: code-splitting plan. Check: Splitting happens after, never before, image and script fixes are measured..
9. **Delay non-critical CSS and complete critical CSS.** Delays non-critical CSS (modals, drawers, filters) with the media=print trick, and makes inlined critical CSS complete per visually distinct template. Skill: `none`. Output: developer brief. Check: Every visually distinct template has its own complete critical CSS block..
10. **Unload unused styles on WooCommerce.** Unloads cart/checkout/block styles from pages without a cart on WooCommerce. Skill: `none`. Output: developer brief. Check: Pages without a cart no longer load cart-related CSS..

Outputs: image fix list with before/after sizes, font fix list, code-splitting plan, developer brief. QA: Before/after byte counts shown for every image fix, not just a claim.; Font swap tested visually, not just by rule.; No critical CSS missing for any visually different template.. Read point: Measured before/after with speed_lab.measure; re-checked at the next monthly regression pass.. Duration: agent: 2-4 hours diagnosis; developer: 1-2 days to implement..

### SITE-W14 Govern scripts and tune caching, CDN and headless performance

Trigger: monthly | on demand. Human role: Speed engineer, infrastructure. Capabilities: SITE-C23, SITE-C28, SITE-C31. Needs: connector: shopify admin, connector: hosting provider, results/serve.log, custom site repo (if headless).

Output: delay/removal plan. Check: Every deferred script's feature was tested working after the change..
3. **Use the platform's own delay mechanism.** On Shopify, uses the content_for_header delay override rather than commenting a script out; never changes pixel loading without testing every event on and off. Skill: `none`. Output: delay/removal plan. Check: Every tracking event tested on and off before shipping..
4. **Scope app assets to the right templates.** Loads app assets only on the templates where the widget appears, and turns off unused app embeds. Skill: `site/speed-audit/infra-and-scripts`. Output: developer brief. Check: No app script loads on a template that doesn't use it..
5. **Verify caching from response headers.** Verifies caching and compression directly from response headers, not a plugin's settings page. Skill: `none`. Output: caching configuration. Check: Headers checked directly with a real request, not read from an admin screen..
6. **Confirm visitor-specific pages are never cached.** Confirms cart, checkout, account and any visitor-specific page are never cached. Skill: `none`. Output: caching configuration. Check: A live check confirms no cache header on a visitor-specific page..
7. **Cache static assets and target CDN hit rate.** Caches static assets long-term with versioned filenames and sets up a CDN targeting an 80%+ hit rate. Skill: `none`. Output: caching configuration. Check: CDN hit rate measured and reported, not assumed..
8. **Write the host brief after front-end fixes are measured.** Writes a host brief with the 4 key questions only after front-end fixes are already measured, and matches hosting tier to real order volume. Skill: `shared/developer-brief`. Output: host brief with 4 questions. Check: Brief is written after, not before, front-end fixes are measured..
9. **Prioritize the true LCP image and server-render the buy box.** On a headless or framework storefront, marks only the true LCP image as priority and server-renders the buy box and top collection tiles. Skill: `site/speed-audit/infra-and-scripts`. Output: framework performance plan. Check: Only one image per template is marked priority..
10. **Trim API payloads and code-split UI.** Returns only the fields a tile or page actually needs from the API, and code-splits/lazy-loads non-critical UI components. Skill: `none`. Output: pull request. Check: API payload size measured before and after the trim..

Outputs: script inventory, delay/removal plan, caching configuration, host brief with 4 questions, framework performance plan, pull request. QA: Every tracking event tested on and off before shipping a script change.; No visitor-specific page ever cached.; Host brief asks real questions, not guesses at server settings.. Read point: Re-measured after each change; host brief reviewed at the next hosting renewal or when speed budget is missed.. Duration: agent: 3-4 hours; developer: 1-2 days for headless-specific fixes..

### SITE-W15 Write speed briefs, run regression monitoring and prepare for peak load

Trigger: on demand | monthly | event: upcoming sale. Human role: Speed programme manager. Capabilities: SITE-C32, SITE-C33, SITE-C29. Needs: diagnosed fixes from SITE-W12 through SITE-W14, results/*.json history, brand brain: upcoming sale calendar.

1. **Turn each diagnosed fix into a brief.** Turns every diagnosed fix into a brief with what/needs/tradeoff/who, a pass threshold tied to a real metric, and a one-step rollback. Skill: `shared/developer-brief`. Output: developer brief (per fix). Check: Every threshold is a number, not a feeling..
2. **Share the report link and sequence fixes.** Shares the PageSpeed report's own link instead of relaying findings by hand, and sequences fixes content-before-infrastructure. Skill: `none`. Output: developer brief (per fix). Check: Sequencing places content fixes before infrastructure fixes..
3. **Write the founder decision page.** Writes a founder decision page bundling every fix, using speed_lab.build_founder_page's format. Skill: `site/speed-audit`. Output: founder decision page. Check: Page uses the standard build_founder_page shape, not an ad hoc format..
4. **Re-audit monthly against a budget.** Re-audits speed on a monthly schedule, holding a page-weight and script-count budget as its own tracked number. Skill: `site/speed-audit`. Output: monthly speed trend report. Check: Script count is tracked as its own number, separate from page weight..
5. **Gate new features on a speed check.** Runs a speed check on any new feature or app before it ships, gated into the deploy pipeline. Skill: `none`. Output: regression alert. Check: No new feature ships without a pre-ship speed check..
6. **Wait for field data before judging.** Waits about 28 days of field data before judging a fix worked, reading field (CrUX) and lab data as separate things. Skill: `none`. Output: monthly speed trend report. Check: A fix is not called a win before 28 days of field data exist..
7. **Flag a regression the moment it appears.** Flags a regression the moment page weight or script count crosses budget, and logs the monthly trend. Skill: `none`. Output: regression alert. Check: Alert fires within the same monthly cycle the budget is crossed, not the next one..
8. **List at-risk dynamic steps before a sale.** Lists every dynamic step (search, filters, cart, checkout, quiz) at risk of a peak-event spike ahead of a scheduled sale. Skill: `none`. Output: peak readiness report. Check: Every dynamic step on the funnel is represented on the list..
9. **Load-test and consider a waiting room.** Load-tests the at-risk steps before the event, and considers a virtual waiting room for a limited-inventory drop. Skill: `none`. Output: peak readiness report. Check: Load test run at a multiple of expected peak traffic, not just current traffic.. Ask the founder if: the drop's expected traffic is unknown or unprecedented.
10. **Freeze changes during the peak window.** Freezes app installs and theme changes during the peak window, with a tested rollback path kept ready. Skill: `none`. Output: change freeze notice. Check: Freeze window covers the entire sale, confirmed with the founder..

Outputs: developer brief (per fix), founder decision page, monthly speed trend report, regression alert, peak readiness report, change freeze notice. QA: Every brief's threshold is a number, not a feeling.; Regression alert fires the moment a budget is crossed, not weeks later.; Freeze window covers the entire sale, confirmed with the founder.. Read point: Monthly trend; immediate alert on regression; readiness confirmed 1-2 weeks before a known sale.. Duration: agent: 2-3 hours monthly; load-test runs: hours, scheduled ahead of the event..

### SITE-W16 Design traffic routing, funnel architecture and new-channel readiness

Trigger: event: new campaign or SKU launch | new landing page requested, or a page-builder app named as a speed-audit root cause | agent-driven orders start appearing in order source data. Human role: Funnel architect. Capabilities: SITE-C35, SITE-C61, SITE-C73. Needs: brand brain: avatar and awareness stage, brand brain: ad performance (read-only), page inventory, current app list.

1. **Classify awareness stage and price point.** Classifies the SKU's traffic awareness stage and price point before picking a page type. Skill: `site/funnel-architecture`. Output: page-type recommendation per campaign. Check: Classification is stated with the evidence behind it, not asserted..
2. **Route by intent and skepticism.** Sends high-intent or simple-product traffic straight to the PDP, and puts an educating page between the ad and PDP for cold or skeptical traffic. Skill: `none`. Output: funnel architecture diagram. Check: Routing choice matches the traffic source's real intent signal, not a default..
3. **Build advertorials and campaign pages only when earned.** Builds the advertorial only after a winning ad or written research already exists, and builds a dedicated single-product campaign page for paid traffic. Skill: `site/funnel-architecture`. Output: funnel architecture diagram. Check: No advertorial commissioned without a prior winning ad or research base..
4. **Decide the build method by speed budget and skill.** Decides how the page gets built: native theme Liquid, an AI page-builder app, or a headless page, driven by the page's speed budget and the team's coding skill. Skill: `none`. Output: builder decision memo. Check: Decision states the speed cost of each option, not just feature lists..
5. **Weigh builder app weight against buying another app.** Lists the JS/CSS weight each candidate builder adds, measured on the brand's own speed profile, and weighs built-in features (A/B testing, analytics, personalization) against buying a separate app. Skill: `site/funnel-architecture`. Output: app weight comparison table. Check: Weight numbers come from the brand's own speed profile, not a vendor's claim..
6. **Flag and migrate a heavy-builder page.** Flags when a page already built in a heavy builder is the diagnosed cause of a speed regression, and writes the migration brief to native code. Skill: `none`. Output: migration brief for flagged pages. Check: Migration only proposed when a builder is a proven, measured root cause..
7. **Verify the AI-agent-readable product feed.** Verifies the product feed and structured data a third-party AI shopping assistant would read (price, stock, variant, shipping) are accurate and current. Skill: `site/funnel-architecture/synthetic-validation`. Output: agentic-checkout readiness report. Check: Feed data checked against the live PDP, not assumed synced..
8. **Check the checkout completes an agent-initiated purchase.** Checks the checkout can complete an agent-initiated purchase without a broken step, and confirms order attribution correctly tags it. Skill: `none`. Output: attribution tag fix. Check: An agent-tagged order is never miscounted as organic or paid..
9. **Flag blocking steps for the founder.** Flags any step (CAPTCHA, mandatory account) blocking an agent purchase and decides with the founder whether to relax it. Skill: `none`. Output: blocked-step list. Check: Every flagged step includes the fraud tradeoff of relaxing it.. Ask the founder if: relaxing a checkout gate has a real fraud tradeoff.

Outputs: funnel architecture diagram, page-type recommendation per campaign, builder decision memo, app weight comparison table, migration brief for flagged pages, agentic-checkout readiness report. QA: Page-type choice matches the SKU's real awareness stage, not a default.; Builder choice backed by a measured JS/CSS weight number.; Agentic-checkout readiness kept as read-only measurement until real orders are seen.. Read point: At every new campaign or SKU launch; agentic-checkout revisited when order-source data shows agent-driven orders.. Duration: agent: half a day per campaign; builder migration: 1-3 days if flagged..

### SITE-W17 Run pre-build research, message-match audit and shopper simulation

Trigger: event: before any new page build | event: ad creative changes | before a redesign goes to a real A/B test. Human role: Page researcher. Capabilities: SITE-C48, SITE-C36, SITE-C66. Needs: connector: ga4, connector: session recording tool, connector: reviews platform, mcp: meta ads (read-only).

1. **Pull the SKU's own funnel data.** Pulls that SKU's own funnel data, not a site average, and finds the weakest funnel stage to fix first. Skill: `site/funnel-architecture/pre-build-research`. Output: research brief. Check: Data pulled is specific to this SKU, not a blended site number..
2. **Read scroll maps and watch converted sessions.** Reads scroll maps mobile-first at the fold, 75%, 50% and 25% lines, and watches converted-session recordings first, then rage clicks. Skill: `none`. Output: research brief. Check: Recordings watched are converted sessions first, not a random sample..
3. **Mine reviews and read the awareness signal.** Mines reviews and surveys into ranked topics, and reads the market's awareness stage from search-trend data. Skill: `none`. Output: research brief. Check: Topics are ranked, not just listed..
4. **Write the 1-page brief.** Writes the 1-page brief: audience, pain, outcome, awareness stage, page type, triggering event. Skill: `site/funnel-architecture/pre-build-research`. Output: 1-page build brief. Check: The brief fits on one page and names a triggering event..
5. **Mirror the ad's exact promise in the hero.** Mirrors the live ad's exact promise in the page's hero headline and continues its story rather than starting a new one. Skill: `site/funnel-architecture/message-match-audit`. Output: message-match audit report. Check: Headline wording traces directly to the specific ad's promise..
6. **Keep persona, price and guarantee identical end to end.** Keeps one persona and matches price, discount, guarantee and shipping identically from ad to page to cart to checkout. Skill: `none`. Output: mismatch fix list. Check: Every mismatch found is listed with the exact page location..
7. **Re-run the check on every ad change.** Re-runs the message-match check the moment ad creative changes. Skill: `none`. Output: message-match audit report. Check: Re-run happens the same day a live ad is changed, confirmed by timestamp..
8. **Define personas and run them in a sandbox.** Before an expensive redesign, defines 3-6 synthetic shopper personas matching the brand's real segments and runs them through the current site and the candidate design in a private sandbox. Skill: `site/funnel-architecture/synthetic-validation`. Output: persona run report. Check: Personas map to real customer segments, not invented archetypes..
9. **Record friction and compare designs.** Records add-to-cart rate, click-path length and the exact abandonment step per persona, and compares two designs head to head. Skill: `none`. Output: friction points list, design comparison summary. Check: Every persona's abandonment step is named, not summarized as 'some friction'..
10. **Label the simulation as a prediction.** States plainly that the simulation is a prediction, not a measured result, and should not replace a real A/B test where real money is at stake. Skill: `none`. Output: design comparison summary. Check: The report explicitly says 'prediction' and names the real test it should still go through..

Outputs: research brief, 1-page build brief, message-match audit report, mismatch fix list, persona run report, friction points list, design comparison summary. QA: Brief built from that SKU's own data, not a site average.; Message-match re-run every time the feeding ad changes.; Persona simulation labelled as prediction, never presented as a real result.. Read point: Before every new page build; on every ad creative change; before a redesign is committed to build.. Duration: agent: half a day research; simulation run: 1-2 hours in sandbox..

### SITE-W18 Build and score the hero, optimize the PDP buy box

4. **Show 2 proof forms and real FUD reducers.** Shows 2 verifiable proof forms above the fold and places real FUD reducers next to every CTA. Skill: `none`. Output: hero copy draft. Check: Every FUD reducer traces to a real policy, not an invented reassurance..
5. **Choose an image that shows the promise.** Chooses a hero image or video that shows the promise itself, not a generic lifestyle shot. Skill: `none`. Output: hero layout spec. Check: Image or video directly depicts the promised outcome..

7. **Place controls next to what they change.** Places every control next to the element it changes, and adds a subscribe-and-save selector for consumable SKUs. Skill: `none`. Output: PDP buy-box spec. Check: Subscribe-and-save only appears on genuinely consumable SKUs..
8. **Show delivery ETA and local payment trust.** Shows delivery ETA or a pincode serviceability check near the buy box, and local payment methods beside the FUD reducers. Skill: `none`. Output: PDP buy-box spec. Check: Delivery ETA is computed from real courier data, not a flat promise..
9. **Answer the 4 above-the-fold questions.** Answers what, why this one, why now and why trust you above the fold, with reviews and gallery placed next to the buy box. Skill: `none`. Output: before/after mock. Check: All 4 questions are answerable from the above-the-fold content alone..

Outputs: hero copy draft, hero layout spec, PDP buy-box spec, before/after mock. QA: Headline names the outcome, not the category.; First-screen fits on the brand's actual mobile device, tested not assumed.; Every claim in the hero traces to the proof file.. Read point: Founder approves before build; re-scored if the feeding ad or offer changes.. Duration: agent: half a day per page..

4. **Map proof to objections and scatter it.** Maps every testimonial, badge and press quote to the one objection it answers, and scatters proof through the page instead of stacking it once. Skill: `none`. Output: proof placement map. Check: Every proof element is tagged to one named objection..

6. **Build a post-purchase proof survey.** Builds a short post-purchase survey to keep collecting proof on purpose instead of waiting for it to arrive. Skill: `none`. Output: post-purchase survey questions. Check: Survey questions map to the objections the page still needs proof for..
7. **Write true, risk-framed urgency.** Uses urgency and scarcity only when true and phrased as risk, never a fake count, and wires a real-time low-stock display to live inventory. 8. **Present the guarantee word-for-word.** Presents the guarantee as confidence, matched word-for-word to policy, and closes long pages with 2 honest options: leave, or try risk-free. Skill: `none`. Output: guarantee copy block. Check: Guarantee wording matches the policy page exactly..

10. **Flag and propose the corrected number.** Flags a claim as stale the moment the cited competitor price moves enough to make the comparison inaccurate, and proposes the corrected number for a human to approve. Skill: `shared/claim-check`. Output: proposed corrected comparison copy. Check: No comparison copy changes without human approval..

Outputs: page copy draft, FAQ block, proof placement map, post-purchase survey questions, urgency/scarcity copy, guarantee copy block, stale-claim flags, proposed corrected comparison copy. QA: No urgency or scarcity claim without a true, current basis.; Guarantee copy matches policy word-for-word.; No comparison claim ships without a current, dated competitor source.. ### SITE-W20 Remove friction, QA pages mobile-first and audit accessibility

Trigger: on demand | event: before any page launch | quarterly. Human role: Page friction, mobile and accessibility QA lead. Capabilities: SITE-C43, SITE-C44, SITE-C59. Needs: brand brain: device split (from GA4), theme code, key templates (home, PDP, collection, cart, checkout).

1. **Strip navigation on single-offer pages.** Strips navigation to logo, cart and one CTA on single-offer campaign pages. Skill: `none`. Output: friction fix list. Check: No competing link remains on a single-offer page..
2. **Open a cart drawer, not a reload.** Opens a cart drawer on add-to-cart instead of reloading, and fires pop-ups only on real intent signals, never in the first 3-5 seconds. Skill: `site/mobile-first-qa`. Output: friction fix list. Check: Pop-up timing tested against an intent signal, not a flat timer..
3. **Fix dead controls and remove unused promo boxes.** Fixes or removes false affordances and dead controls, and removes the cart discount-code box when no promotion is live. Skill: `none`. Output: friction fix list. Check: Every visible control does something when clicked, tested live..
4. **Repair blockers and stage form fields.** Repairs popups, chat widgets or cookie bars covering add-to-cart, and reveals extra form fields only after the first is filled. Skill: `site/mobile-first-qa`. Output: developer brief. Check: Add-to-cart never visually obstructed on a real device screenshot..
5. **Build and review every page mobile-first.** Builds and reviews every page mobile-first, on the brand's own real device split, checking every screen width in responsive mode. Skill: `none`. Output: mobile QA report. Check: Reviewed on the brand's real device split, not a generic phone size..
6. **Pin the CTA and never hide content in a carousel.** Pins the PDP CTA on mobile with live price without covering the variant selector, and never hides reviews or content behind a mobile-only carousel. Skill: `none`. Output: screenshot set per width. Check: No content that appears on desktop is hidden behind a carousel on mobile..
7. **Cap mobile forms and prioritize mobile effort.** Cuts mobile forms to 4 fields or fewer with visible labels, and puts about 90% of CRO effort on mobile. Skill: `site/mobile-first-qa`. Output: mobile QA report. Check: No mobile form exceeds 4 fields without a stated reason..
8. **Run an automated WCAG scan.** Runs an automated WCAG scan on home, PDP, collection, cart and checkout templates. Skill: `site/mobile-first-qa`. Output: accessibility audit report. Check: All 5 key templates scanned, not just the homepage..
9. **Manually test with keyboard and screen reader.** Manually tests forms, navigation and dynamic states with a keyboard and a screen reader, separating real failures from automated false positives. Skill: `none`. Output: accessibility audit report. Check: Manual pass included, not the automated scan alone..
10. **Scope remediation to the theme codebase.** Closes cookie-consent and data-privacy gaps alongside accessibility ones, and scopes every remediation to the theme codebase so fixes survive updates. Skill: `none`. Output: developer brief. Check: Remediation lives in the theme codebase, re-scanned after the next theme change..

Outputs: friction fix list, developer brief, mobile QA report, screenshot set per width, accessibility audit report. QA: Every friction fix tested live, not assumed from a screenshot.; Mobile QA run on the brand's own device split, not a generic phone.; Accessibility remediation lives in the theme codebase, re-scanned after the next theme change.. Read point: Before every page launch; accessibility re-audited quarterly and after any theme change.. Duration: agent: half a day for friction/mobile QA; accessibility: 1 day including manual testing..
