### SITE-W22 Design, build and route a quiz funnel

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Quiz funnel builder. Capabilities: SITE-C46. Needs: brand brain: products, brand brain: sizing/consumption data.

1. **Cut questions with no clear job.** Cuts every candidate question that has no clear job in routing or personalizing the result. Skill: `none`. Output: quiz spec (questions, branching, tracking). Check: Every remaining question can be justified by its downstream use..

4. **Insert real information screens.** Inserts a real information screen about every 3-5 questions, never a filler screen. Skill: `none`. Output: quiz spec. Check: Every info screen carries real, useful content..

6. **Route each answer to its offer.** Routes each answer combination to its matching page or offer. Skill: `none`. Output: results page spec. Check: No answer combination is left without a mapped destination..

8. **QA every branching path.** QAs the branching logic end to end for every realistic answer path. Skill: `none`. Output: quiz spec. Check: Every realistic path tested at least once..

Outputs: quiz spec (questions, branching, tracking), results page spec. QA: No dead-end branch with no matching offer.; Completion rate and purchase rate both tracked, not just completion.; No question included without a clear job.. Read point: Founder reviews the full spec before build; completion and purchase rates read 2 weeks after launch.. Duration: agent: 1 day for spec; build: 2-4 days depending on the quiz app..

### SITE-W24 Run the CRO test lifecycle: design, launch, read, roll out and protect

Trigger: on demand | event: before test launch | event: test reaches read point | quarterly. Human role: CRO analyst. Capabilities: SITE-C50, SITE-C51, SITE-C52, SITE-C53, SITE-C54. Needs: ranked backlog (from Propose/registry), traffic and order volume, metric contract, registry: prior test results on this brand.

1. **Check the traffic gate.** Checks the traffic gate: below the sample-size threshold, plans a bigger change instead of running more small tests. Skill: `site/cro-test-lifecycle`. Output: test design spec. Check: Traffic gate check happens before any test is designed, not after it underperforms..
2. **Size the test from the minimum detectable effect.** Sizes the test from the minimum detectable effect, stated as orders per variant, and computes runtime from arms x sample / weekly traffic, at least 2 full weeks covering each weekday twice. Skill: `none`. Output: test design spec. Check: Sample size is stated as orders per variant, never a flat magic number..
3. **Set the stopping rule before launch.** Picks a stopping rule before launch and never peeks outside planned looks; randomizes by user with sticky bucketing and isolates returning-visitor traffic. Skill: `site/cro-test-lifecycle/stats-design`. Output: test design spec. Check: Stopping rule is written down before the test goes live..
4. **QA the test on real devices.** QAs the test 2-3 times on real devices, confirms tracking fires in both arms, and checks for flicker on a slow connection. Skill: `site/cro-test-lifecycle/launch-qa`. Output: launch QA checklist. Check: Tracking confirmed firing in both arms before launch..
5. **Check the promo calendar and run an A/A if needed.** Checks the promo calendar for overlap, and runs an A/A test when starting a new programme or switching tools. Skill: `none`. Output: go/no-go. Check: No test launches into an unnoticed promo overlap.. Ask the founder if: a promo overlaps the planned test window.
6. **Count conversions on a confirmation event.** At the read point, counts conversions on a confirmation event, never clicks, and never calls a test on one good or bad day. Skill: `site/cro-test-lifecycle/result-read`. Output: result read report. Check: Conversion counted on a real confirmation event, not a click proxy..
7. **Check SRM, novelty and device splits.** Checks sample ratio mismatch, novelty/primacy with a daily plot, and the mobile-only and desktop-only splits before trusting the average. Skill: `none`. Output: result read report. Check: SRM checked and cleared before any result is trusted..
8. **Decide from the interval and business value.** Applies Twyman's law to any surprising win, and decides from the confidence interval and business value together, never p<0.05 alone. Skill: `site/cro-test-lifecycle/result-read`. Output: ship/hold/kill recommendation. Check: Decision cites both the interval and the business value, not a p-value alone..
9. **Ship the winner and re-verify.** Ships the winner as the new control, re-verifies it with fresh recordings and funnel data after shipping, and runs an LTV/returns check before scaling any angle- or claim-driven win. Skill: `site/cro-test-lifecycle/rollout-and-protect`. Output: winner register entry. Check: Re-verification happens after shipping, not assumed from the test result alone..
10. **Maintain the live-winner register.** Keeps a live-winner register and re-checks quarterly that every shipped winner is still live, setting founder expectations at a 10-20% win rate for PDP/cart/checkout tests. Skill: `none`. Output: quarterly protection check report. Check: Every past winner is re-checked live at least once a quarter..
11. **Report programme value from a holdout.** When measuring a feature, popup or the whole programme, reports value from a holdout instead of its own engagement metric or the sum of test lifts. Skill: `site/cro-test-lifecycle/holdout-incrementality`. Output: programme value report. Check: Programme value is never reported as the sum of individual test lifts..
12. **State and challenge the identifying assumption.** States and challenges the identifying assumption before trusting any causal method, and checks the traffic gate before choosing a holdout design. Skill: `none`. Output: holdout design. Check: The identifying assumption is written down and challenged, not assumed..
13. **Steady noisy revenue tests.** Turns on CUPED only for returning, identified users, and winsorizes outlier orders or uses a proportion metric to steady a noisy revenue test. Skill: `none`. Output: holdout design. Check: A noisy revenue metric is steadied with a named technique, not ignored..
14. **Log the full chain to the registry.** Logs the full test-to-result-to-protection chain to the registry for the next backlog cycle. Skill: `shared/registry-entry`. Output: registry entry. Check: Every test's full lifecycle is retrievable from the registry..

Outputs: test design spec, launch QA checklist, result read report, ship/hold/kill recommendation, winner register entry, quarterly protection check report, holdout design, programme value report. QA: No verdict from a p-value alone.; No peeking outside the planned looks.; A shipped winner re-verified quarterly, not assumed permanent.; Holdout design states its identifying assumption in writing.. Read point: At the pre-set read point (never earlier); quarterly for winner protection and holdout measurement.. Duration: agent: design and QA, half a day; test runtime: 2+ weeks; read and rollout, half a day..

### SITE-W25 Optimize on-site search and build visual search

Trigger: on demand | on-site search bottleneck diagnosed and the catalogue is visually distinct enough to benefit. Human role: Search and discovery optimizer. Capabilities: SITE-C56, SITE-C76. Needs: connector: shopify admin | woocommerce, search app (Klevu/Searchanise-class), product photo catalogue.

1. **Fill filter fields and turn filters on.** Fills every field a filter needs and turns on search and discovery filters wired to real product fields. Skill: `site/discovery-and-personalization`. Output: search configuration spec. Check: Every filter tested returns matching products live..
2. **Add predictive text and synonyms.** Improves search with predictive text and synonym matching. Skill: `none`. Output: search configuration spec. Check: A misspelled or synonym query still returns the right product..
3. **Audit search as its own funnel.** Audits search as its own funnel: query volume, no-results rate, and autocomplete lag treated as an everyday interactivity issue. Skill: `site/discovery-and-personalization`. Output: no-results report. Check: No-results rate is tracked as its own number, not folded into overall bounce rate..
4. **Build and maintain the visual similarity index.** Embeds every live product's real photos once and keeps the similarity index current as the catalogue changes. Skill: `none`. Output: similarity index. Check: Index refresh runs whenever the catalogue changes, not on a stale one-time build..
5. **Return real SKUs from an uploaded photo.** Accepts a shopper-uploaded photo, embeds it the same way, and returns the nearest real SKUs, never a generated or redrawn product. Skill: `site/discovery-and-personalization/visual-search`. Output: visual search widget. Check: Every returned result is a real, current SKU photo..
6. **Cap claims and fall back cleanly.** Caps the feature to product discovery only, never claims a visual match is exact, and falls back cleanly to text search when no close match exists. Skill: `none`. Output: visual search widget. Check: No visual match is ever labelled 'exact'.. Ask the founder if: the catalogue is not visually distinct enough to expect a real lift.
7. **Protect uploaded shopper photos.** Keeps uploaded shopper photos private and short-lived, never stored as training or competitor data. Skill: `none`. Output: similarity index. Check: No uploaded shopper photo persists past the session..

Outputs: search configuration spec, no-results report, visual search widget, similarity index. QA: Every filter tested to confirm it returns matching products live.; Visual search never claims an exact match.; No shopper-uploaded photo retained beyond the session.. Read point: Search funnel numbers reviewed monthly; visual search usage reviewed after the first month live.. Duration: agent: 1 day for search audit; visual search build: several days, external model dependent..

### SITE-W26 Design on-site personalization and real-time computed offers

Trigger: on demand | a PDP has enough session volume and an unresolved add-to-cart drop-off | an exit-intent offer is underperforming. Human role: Personalization and CRO data designer. Capabilities: SITE-C57, SITE-C74, SITE-C75. Needs: brand brain: quiz/behavioural data, click-map or session-replay data per PDP, landed cost per SKU, founder's margin floor.

1. **Show the shopper's own answers back.** Shows the shopper's own quiz answers as a personalized progress visual, and recommends a starter pack size from quiz or consumption answers. Skill: `site/discovery-and-personalization/personalization-rules`. Output: personalization rule set. Check: Every personalized element is sourced from that shopper's own real answers..
2. **Match imagery to the selected variant.** Updates the product image to match the selected variant, and branches the post-quiz email sequence by the shopper's own answers. Skill: `none`. Output: personalization rule set. Check: Image updates correctly for every variant option in a live test..
3. **Layer real-time intent signals honestly.** Layers real-time intent signals into reassurance copy, not only past-browsing history, and tailors PDP facts for AI-referred visitors so they do not re-browse. Skill: `none`. Output: developer brief. Check: No personalization rule invents a signal the site does not actually have..
4. **Pull real zoom and dwell data.** Pulls per-image zoom, dwell and skip events from the site's own click-map or session-replay data, never a generic benchmark. Skill: `site/discovery-and-personalization/computed-in-code-offers`. Output: proposed image order with reasoning. Check: Data pulled is this PDP's own, not an industry benchmark..
5. **Rank images by real attention.** Ranks the existing image set by which images hold attention longest before a scroll-away or an add-to-cart. Skill: `none`. Output: proposed image order with reasoning. Check: Ranking is computed from real events, not a stylistic guess..
6. **Propose the re-sequenced gallery for approval.** Proposes a re-sequenced gallery order with the reasoning shown per swap, flagging when session volume is too thin to trust the ranking. Skill: `none`. Output: confidence flag per SKU. Check: Proposal goes to the founder gate, never ships automatically.. Ask the founder if: session volume on this SKU is too thin to trust a ranking.
7. **Read real cost data at the moment of exit intent.** Reads landed cost, shipping cost and any already-applied promo for the exact cart at the moment of exit intent. Skill: `site/discovery-and-personalization/computed-in-code-offers`. Output: per-cart discount ceiling. Check: Cost inputs read at the moment of the cart, not a stale average..
8. **Compute the discount ceiling in code.** Computes, in code, the maximum discount that cart can take and still clear the founder's margin floor; never lets the model estimate the number. Skill: `none`. Output: exit-intent offer config. Check: The number is produced by code from real cost data, never a model guess..
9. **Show the ceiling logic and fail safe.** Shows the founder the ceiling logic before turning the offer live, and falls back to no discount, not a guessed one, when a cost input is missing. Skill: `none`. Output: exit-intent offer config. Check: Missing-cost carts get no discount, never a guessed one..

Outputs: personalization rule set, developer brief, proposed image order with reasoning, confidence flag per SKU, per-cart discount ceiling, exit-intent offer config. QA: Every personalization rule sourced from real shopper data, never invented.; Discount ceiling always computed in code from real cost data, never guessed.; Image re-sequencing proposal goes to the founder gate, never ships automatically.. Read point: Founder gate before any rule or offer goes live; re-checked when session volume or margin floor changes.. Duration: agent: half a day per feature; discount ceiling logic is reusable once built..

### SITE-W27 Design reviews and UGC display strategy

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Reviews and proof strategist. Capabilities: SITE-C58. Needs: brand brain: reviews platform, connector: reviews app.

1. **Pick a reviews app and request past reviews.** Picks a reviews app and requests reviews from past customers before launch if none exist yet. Skill: `site/reviews-ugc-strategy`. Output: reviews configuration. Check: A real request has gone out to past customers, not just the app installed..
2. **Decide the verified-purchase policy.** Decides deliberately whether reviews require a verified purchase, and documents the reason. Skill: `none`. Output: display strategy spec. Check: The decision and its reason are written down, not left implicit..
3. **Build volume, then shift sort order.** Builds review volume first below 100 reviews per product, then shifts sort order to relevance and media-first once past that. Skill: `none`. Output: display strategy spec. Check: Sort order strategy matches the product's actual review count..
4. **Check for suspicious date clustering.** Checks review dates for suspicious clustering before trusting a spike as real. Skill: `site/reviews-ugc-strategy`. Output: display strategy spec. Check: Any suspicious cluster is flagged before the reviews are used as proof..
5. **Show repeat-buyer reviews as continuity proof.** Shows reviews from repeat buyers as continuity proof specifically for consumable products. Skill: `none`. Output: display strategy spec. Check: Repeat-buyer status is verified, not assumed from review text..
6. **Confirm the request automation is sending.** Confirms review-request automation is actually sending by checking a real recent order's timeline. Skill: `none`. Output: reviews configuration. Check: A real order's timeline shows the review request actually fired..

Outputs: reviews configuration, display strategy spec. QA: Verified-purchase policy stated and consistent, not ad hoc.; A suspicious review-date cluster is checked before it is trusted as proof.; Review-request automation confirmed sending on a real order.. Read point: Reviewed quarterly alongside the accessibility and hygiene cadence.. Duration: agent: half a day..

### SITE-W28 Reduce COD return-to-origin and add a WhatsApp buy flow for India

Trigger: COD share and RTO rate identified as a bottleneck in the metric contract | India market COD or trust-driven checkout hesitation diagnosed as a bottleneck. Human role: India operations lead, COD and WhatsApp. Capabilities: SITE-C67, SITE-C77. Needs: courier serviceability API, WhatsApp business API access, brand's historical RTO data.

1. **Show real-time PIN serviceability.** Shows real-time PIN code serviceability on the PDP, using the courier or aggregator API, before the shopper adds to cart. Skill: `site/india-ops`. Output: PDP serviceability widget. Check: Serviceability check reflects the live courier API, not a static list..
2. **Add OTP verification on high-value COD.** Adds address auto-fill and phone OTP verification at checkout for COD orders above a set value. Skill: `none`. Output: checkout OTP flow. Check: OTP is required only above the set value threshold, confirmed live..
3. **Add a visible, sized COD fee.** Adds a visible COD handling fee as a checkout line item, sized to filter impulse orders without losing genuine buyers. Skill: `site/india-ops`. Output: COD fee config. Check: Fee amount is backed by real impulse-order data, not a round guess..
4. **Surface a prepaid incentive at the right moment.** Surfaces a prepaid discount or free-shipping incentive at the exact checkout step where COD is selected. Skill: `none`. Output: checkout OTP flow. Check: Incentive appears at the COD-selection step, not buried earlier..
5. **Trigger a WhatsApp confirmation with auto-cancel.** Triggers an automated WhatsApp order-confirmation message after COD checkout, with an auto-cancel rule if unconfirmed within a set window. Skill: `none`. Output: WhatsApp confirmation flow. Check: Auto-cancel window is enforced and logged..
6. **Replace tracking with a branded page.** Replaces the generic courier tracking link with a branded tracking page that also carries post-purchase upsell content. Skill: `none`. Output: branded tracking page. Check: Tracking page renders correctly on the brand's own domain..
7. **Pass a risk score to fulfilment.** Passes a buyer risk score (first order, high COD value, prior RTO history) to the fulfilment system for a manual check, without running the logistics NDR process itself. Skill: `none`. Output: RTO dashboard. Check: Risk score reaches the fulfilment system without Site running the logistics process.. Ask the founder if: no fulfilment system is ready to receive the risk score.
8. **Build the RTO analytics view.** Builds an RTO analytics view by courier, PIN code, product and payment method. Skill: `none`. Output: RTO dashboard. Check: Dashboard breaks RTO down by all 4 dimensions, not just an overall rate..
9. **Sync the catalogue to WhatsApp.** Syncs the live catalogue (price, stock, variant) to the brand's WhatsApp Business catalog on a schedule. Skill: `none`. Output: synced catalog. Check: Catalog sync runs on a schedule and matches the live PDP..
10. **Add the click-to-WhatsApp button.** Adds a click-to-WhatsApp button on the PDP that pre-fills the exact SKU, variant and a template question, confirmed to appear only where WhatsApp commerce is legal and common. Skill: `site/india-ops/whatsapp-buy-flow`. Output: click-to-WhatsApp button. Check: Button appears only in markets where it is confirmed legal and common..
11. **Measure clicks separately and hand off the conversation.** Measures PDP-to-WhatsApp clicks separately from normal add-to-cart so they are never double-counted, and hands the conversation flow itself to Lifecycle. Skill: `none`. Output: click-to-order attribution. Check: WhatsApp-originated orders never double-count against add-to-cart..

Outputs: PDP serviceability widget, checkout OTP flow, COD fee config, WhatsApp confirmation flow, branded tracking page, RTO dashboard, click-to-WhatsApp button, synced catalog, click-to-order attribution. QA: RTO levers measured against the brand's own PIN-code and payment-method data, never a generic industry number.; WhatsApp click-to-order never double-counted with add-to-cart.; COD fee sized from real impulse-order data, not a guess.. Read point: RTO dashboard reviewed monthly; WhatsApp conversion checked at the same cadence.. Duration: agent: 2-3 days build across both features; ongoing: automated once live..

### SITE-W29 Run the on-site AI shopping assistant

Trigger: event: founder approves; weekly transcript and claims review. Human role: On-site assistant programme owner. Capabilities: SITE-G01. Needs: brand brain: claims tiers (approved/site-backed/forbidden), catalogue, stock and delivery data, founder approval to turn the assistant on.

1. **Confirm approval and current claims tiers.** Confirms the founder has approved turning the assistant on, and confirms the claims tiers are current before go-live. Skill: `site/ai-shopping-assistant`. Output: go-live confirmation. Check: Claims tiers reviewed within the last 30 days before go-live.. Ask the founder if: claims tiers have not been reviewed in the last 30 days.
2. **Restrict answers to approved data.** Configures the assistant to answer only from the catalogue, stock and claims-tier data, never from open web knowledge about the brand. Skill: `none`. Output: assistant configuration. Check: A test question outside the catalogue returns a hand-off, not a guess..
3. **Set the hand-off rule.** Sets the rule that any question the assistant cannot answer from approved data routes to a person, never a guess. Skill: `none`. Output: assistant configuration. Check: Hand-off triggers correctly on an out-of-scope test question..
4. **Keep this surface scoped.** Keeps this pre-purchase chat distinct from the reporting or CRO assistant surfaces so its scope stays fixed. Skill: `none`. Output: assistant configuration. Check: Assistant never answers a reporting or CRO-analysis question..
5. **Log conversations and flag forbidden-tier touches.** Logs every conversation and flags any answer that touched a forbidden-tier claim. Skill: `shared/claim-check`. Output: conversation log. Check: Every forbidden-tier touch is flagged automatically, not found by manual search..
6. **Run the weekly transcript and claims review.** Runs the weekly transcript and claims review, checking a sample of conversations against the claims tiers. Skill: `none`. Output: weekly transcript and claims review report. Check: A real sample of conversations is checked every week, not skipped..
7. **Report hand-off rate and unanswered questions.** Reports hand-off rate, common unanswered questions and any claims-tier miss to the founder each week. Skill: `none`. Output: hand-off rate report. Check: Report is delivered weekly without fail..
8. **Feed unanswered questions into page copy.** Feeds unanswered-question patterns back as candidate FAQ content for the page-copy workflow. Skill: `none`. Output: candidate FAQ list. Check: Candidate FAQ items are handed to SITE-W19, not left unused..
9. **Pause immediately on a violation.** Pauses the assistant and notifies the founder immediately if a claims-tier violation is found, rather than waiting for the weekly review. Skill: `none`. Output: go-live confirmation. Check: A found violation pauses the assistant same-day, not at the next weekly review..

Outputs: weekly transcript and claims review report, hand-off rate report, candidate FAQ list. QA: No answer given outside the catalogue, stock and claims-tier data.; Every claims-tier miss surfaced immediately, not held for the weekly review.; Hand-off to a person happens on any question outside approved data.. Read point: Weekly transcript review; immediate pause on any violation.. Duration: agent: continuous; human: weekly review, 30-45 minutes..

### SITE-W30 Run festive gifting operations

Trigger: event: 60 days before each gifting window (Diwali, Rakhi, wedding season). Human role: Festive gifting operations lead. Capabilities: SITE-G02. Needs: gifting calendar (shared with Lifecycle), brand brain: shipping cutoffs by pincode, brand brain: catalogue.

1. **Confirm the window and cutoff dates.** Confirms the gifting window and its cutoff dates from the shared gifting calendar 60 days out. Skill: `site/festive-gifting-ops`. Output: pincode delivery cutoff map. Check: Cutoff dates confirmed against the shared calendar, not assumed from last year..
2. **Turn on gift wrap and gift messages.** Turns on gift wrap and a gift-message option on the relevant products. Skill: `none`. Output: gift wrap/message configuration. Check: Option appears only on products the founder wants it on..
3. **Build a price-hidden gift invoice.** Builds a price-hidden gift invoice path so a gift recipient never sees the price paid. Skill: `none`. Output: hidden-price invoice flow. Check: A test gift order's packing slip shows no price..
4. **Set delivery cutoffs per pincode.** Sets delivery-by-date cutoffs per pincode so a shopper is never sold a promise the courier cannot keep. Skill: `none`. Output: pincode delivery cutoff map. Check: Cutoff dates match real courier data by pincode.. Ask the founder if: courier cutoff data by pincode is not available or stale.
5. **Build gift guides from real data.** Builds gift guides grouping the catalogue by recipient and occasion, using real product and price data only. Skill: `none`. Output: gift guide pages. Check: No product or price in a gift guide is invented or stale..
6. **Switch to gifting-mode messaging.** Switches relevant PDPs and the homepage to gifting-mode messaging for the window's duration. Skill: `none`. Output: gift guide pages. Check: Messaging switch confirmed live on the first day of the window..
7. **QA the full gift path.** QAs the full gift-wrap-to-hidden-price-to-delivery-cutoff path as a real gift buyer would experience it. Skill: `none`. Output: gifting-mode QA report. Check: A real test order completes the full gift path without a broken step..
8. **Switch back after the window.** Switches everything back to normal mode the day after the window closes. Skill: `none`. Output: gifting-mode QA report. Check: Normal mode confirmed restored the day after close..
9. **Log results for next year.** Logs what worked and the cutoff accuracy for next year's window. Skill: `none`. Output: gifting-mode QA report. Check: Cutoff accuracy and any missed promise are logged for next year's setup..

Outputs: gift wrap/message configuration, hidden-price invoice flow, pincode delivery cutoff map, gift guide pages, gifting-mode QA report. QA: No gift order promises a delivery date the pincode cutoff cannot support.; Hidden-price invoice tested with a real order.; Gifting mode switched off cleanly after the window.. Read point: 60 days before the window (setup); day after close (teardown and log).. Duration: agent: 1-2 days setup; automated switch-on/off at the scheduled dates..

## Paid creative and copy (CREA), 25 workflows

### CREA-W01 Launch a new paid creative test, start to finish

Trigger: an approved card from Propose | on demand. Human role: Performance creative lead running one test from research to the first read. Capabilities: CREA-C03, CREA-C04, CREA-C05, CREA-C06, CREA-C07, CREA-C08, CREA-C11, CREA-C19, CREA-C20, CREA-C22, CREA-C24, CREA-C26, CREA-C31. Needs: brand brain: voice, audience avatars, claim tiers, product facts, approved idea/card record with bottleneck and expected effect, registry: past creative tests and learnings for this persona and angle, metric contract: hook_rate, cvr_overall, cpa, roas_blended, with freshness stamps, Reference Scout access for competitor and swipe material.

1. **Pull the objection map and format scan.** Pulls the brand's existing mined research (voice of customer, owned by the Brand Brain Keeper) plus a fresh check of the account's own last 90 days of ad-level results for which formats currently deliver best (hook rate, hold rate, CPA by format). Skill: `crea/research/objection-and-format-scan`. Output: objection map (each objection quoted and sourced) plus a format scan citing real numbers. Check: every objection has a quoted source and a date; every 'format working now' line cites a real metric, not an impression. Ask the founder if: no support inbox or review source is connected for this brand.
2. **Pull competitor signal for layout and mood.** Pulls 3 to 8 competitor ad references from the Meta gallery's Competitors tab and the Reference Scout, tagged with why each fits this bottleneck. Skill: `crea/research/competitor-signal-pull`. Output: 3 to 8 tagged competitor references, layout and mood only. Check: every reference carries a one-line reason and a 'signal only' tag; no text, logo, product or face is marked for reuse.
3. **Define the angle and dominant appeal.** Turns the objection map, format scan and avatar into 2 to 4 candidate angles, each one sentence, one persona, one dominant appeal, one buying question. Skill: `crea/angle-and-test/angle-builder`. Output: angle brief: 2 to 4 angles, each with persona, appeal, buying question, awareness stage. Check: no angle uses a forbidden-tier claim; each angle names one dominant appeal only.
4. **Write the test plan.** Turns the angle brief into a hypothesis, a clean one-persona-per-ad-set structure, a pack size sized to the account's own delivery volume, and a pre-set winner and kill rule for the creative side. Skill: `crea/angle-and-test/test-plan-builder`. Output: test brief: hypothesis, pack size, winner rule, kill rule, minimum read window. Check: winner and kill rule exist before any script is written; pack size matches this account's real traffic, not a generic default.
5. **Build the audience spec and hand it to MEDIA.** Turns the angle's persona into age range, traits, interests and exclusions matched to the avatar record, and hands the spec to the Media buyer specialist as a structured request. CREA does not build or launch the audience itself. Skill: `crea/angle-and-test/audience-spec-handoff`. Output: audience spec record: age range, traits, exclusions, matched persona id, handed to MEDIA. Check: every trait in the spec ties back to a line in the persona record, not a guess. Ask the founder if: the angle targets a persona with no avatar record yet.
6. **Pick the format and compare format economics.** Chooses the ad format (static, native platform format, UGC video, founder video, carousel, catalogue) from the product's real education or demo need and the account's own expected profit per ad by format, not ROAS alone. Skill: `crea/angle-and-test/format-economics`. Output: format decision with the profit-per-ad comparison behind it. Check: decision cites a number, not a preference.
7. **Write hooks and scripts or copy per format.** Writes the hook bank (8 to 20 hooks, graded on 5 criteria) and the format-specific script or static copy structure for each chosen format. Skill: `crea/write-copy/hooks`. Output: hook bank plus a script or copy draft per format. Check: no product name or offer in a cold hook; one dominant appeal held throughout.
8. **Build the creative brief and production or shoot brief.** Fills the one-page concept brief, then the format-specific production brief (static design brief, UGC or founder-video brief, or catalogue template spec) and generates a first draft image where the format calls for one. Skill: `crea/briefing/concept-brief`. Output: concept brief, production brief, and a draft asset (image or script package). Check: destination link matches the offer stated; hook, image and copy share one awareness stage; real product photo used if fit, material or scale matters.
9. **Run the copy and claim-compliance pass.** Applies the fixed direct-response copywriting rules (reading level, single idea, proof ranking) and then the brand's claim-tier and platform-policy check on every line. Skill: `crea/compliance/copy-craft-rules`. Output: edited copy plus a claim audit table, pass or fail per line. Check: reading level under grade 10; no claim from the forbidden tier; no em dash. Ask the founder if: a claim sits at the edge of the site-backed tier.
10. **Run a feedback and reference round with the Reference Scout.** Sends the draft brief and asset back through one reference round: checks it against the tagged competitor references for layout and mood fit, and routes it for one internal feedback pass before it reaches a human reviewer. Skill: `none`. Output: revised brief or asset with a short note on what changed and why. Check: no competitor text, logo, product or face was copied in the revision.
11. **Apply naming and tagging.** Applies the fixed naming and tagging schema (concept, persona, angle, offer, format, stage, source) to every asset and copy file before it leaves CREA. Skill: `crea/production-ops/naming-and-tagging`. Output: named, tagged asset set. Check: every asset has a stable id following the current schema.
12. **Build the launch checklist and hand off to MEDIA.** Assembles the launch checklist (creative files, copy, audience spec reference, test brief, destination link, tracking check) and hands the full package to the Media buyer specialist for account build and go-live. Skill: `none`. Output: launch checklist and handoff package to MEDIA. Check: every checklist line is checked or has a named blocker; MEDIA confirms receipt.
13. **Read the first-read numbers.** At the test brief's minimum read window, pulls the creative-side metrics (hook rate, hold rate, CPA per ad) from the metric contract and compares each ad to the account average and the test's pre-set winner and kill rule. Skill: `none`. Output: read note: winner, loser or inconclusive, per ad, against the pre-set rule. Check: verdict uses the pre-set rule, not a new judgment call; missing metrics show as unavailable, never zero.
14. **Log the result and close the loop.** Writes the registry entry for this test (angle, format, hook, result, learning) so the next research pass in step 1 starts from it. Skill: `shared/registry-entry`. Output: registry entry. Check: entry links back to the original card or idea id.

Outputs: objection map, angle brief, test brief, audience spec record, hook bank, script or static copy, concept brief, production brief, draft asset, claim audit table, launch checklist, read note, registry entry. QA: every claim traced to an approved or site-backed tier; no competitor text, logo, product or face reused; reading level under grade 10; real product photo used where accuracy matters; winner and kill rule set before launch; every number came from the metric contract, not estimated. Read point: at the test brief's minimum read window (set in step 4 from the account's own delivery volume, not a fixed default). Duration: agent time: half a day to a day across the pipeline; waiting time: the read window itself, typically 3 to 10 days depending on account volume.

### CREA-W02 Script a founder or authority-led ad

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Direct-response scriptwriter briefing a founder or authority-led ad. Capabilities: CREA-C09, CREA-C26. Needs: brand brain: founder's real stories, credentials and permissioned reviews, angle brief or approved idea, claim tiers.

1. **Confirm the founder fact set.** Pulls the founder's or authority's real, permissioned stories, credentials and reviews from the brand brain. Nothing invented. Skill: `none`. Output: founder fact sheet with sources. Check: every fact in the sheet has a source and a date on file. Ask the founder if: a strong story exists but no written permission is on file.
2. **Pick the argument, not a biography.** Frames the ad as an argument for the viewer's problem (claim, proof, mechanism), never a life story, using the angle brief. Skill: `crea/angle-and-test/angle-builder`. Output: argument outline: claim, proof, mechanism. Check: outline states the viewer's problem in the first line, not the founder's history.
3. **Write the script skeleton.** Drafts the scene-by-scene script (hook, bridge, mechanism, product, proof, objection, CTA) sized to the product's real education need. Skill: `crea/write-copy/scripts`. Output: scene-by-scene script table. Check: single CTA; no unverified claim.
4. **Diversify the founder's own sourcing.** Varies delivery style, setting and specific proof point across 2 to 3 script variants so the same founder does not read as one repeated ad. Skill: `crea/make-creatives/founder-video`. Output: 2 to 3 script variants. Check: each variant uses a different real proof point, not a reworded line.
5. **Direct the shoot or AI-touched frame.** Sets shot direction (gaze toward the CTA, setting, product visibility) for a real shoot or an AI-touched frame, keeping the founder's real likeness accurate. Skill: `crea/briefing/shoot-and-photo-direction`. Output: shot list or frame-direction note. Check: founder's real face and product are not redrawn.
6. **Run the copy and claim pass.** Applies copywriting rules then the claim-tier check on every spoken and on-screen line. Skill: `crea/compliance/copy-craft-rules`. Output: audited script. Check: no claim outside the approved or site-backed tier.
7. **Clear rights and hand off.** Confirms any stock element (music, b-roll) used alongside the founder footage is cleared, then hands the finished script package to production or the shoot workflow. Skill: `crea/compliance/rights-clearance`. Output: rights-cleared script package. Check: every non-founder asset in the script has a license on file.

Outputs: founder fact sheet, script variants, rights-cleared script package. QA: no invented founder story; no claim outside approved tiers; single CTA per script. Read point: same read window as the launch workflow once the script ships as an ad. Duration: agent time: 2 to 3 hours; waiting time: none until a shoot is booked.

### CREA-W03 Brief and source a UGC creator, track rights

Trigger: event: rights expiring | weekly. Human role: Creator brief writer and UGC rights tracker. Capabilities: CREA-C10, CREA-C51. Needs: angle brief or bottleneck, creator roster, past creator work and vetting notes, current rights log.

1. **Scan organic mentions for ad-worthy content.** Scans the brand's own tags, mentions and review videos for genuine customer content strong enough to become a paid ad. Skill: `crea/special-formats/organic-ugc-scout`. Output: shortlist of candidate organic clips with source and date. Check: each clip traces to a real public post, not a screenshot with no source. Ask the founder if: no social listening source is connected.
2. **Vet the shortlist.** Checks each candidate against the vetting bar set by past creator work: authenticity, product visibility, tone match. Skill: `none`. Output: vetted shortlist. Check: every kept clip scores above the bar on all three criteria.
3. **Decide wide or specific brief.** Picks a wide, discovery-style brief for a new creator or a specific, replication-style brief when a proven structure already exists. Skill: `crea/make-creatives/ugc-video`. Output: brief type decision. Check: decision states why, not just a default.
4. **Write the creator brief.** Writes the concept, the do and don't say list, and the take allowance. Never sends a creator to 'make content' with no concept. Skill: `crea/make-creatives/ugc-video`. Output: creator brief document. Check: brief names a concept and a specific do/don't list.
5. **Set usage rights and expiry.** Sets the rights scope (paid whitelisting versus organic only), duration and renewal date before any paid use. Skill: `none`. Output: rights terms attached to the brief. Check: expiry date is set and logged before any spend touches this content. Ask the founder if: a creator's ask sits outside the brand's standard rights terms.
6. **Log to the rights tracker.** Writes the rights log entry: creator, content id, scope, expiry, renewal contact. Skill: `shared/registry-entry`. Output: rights log entry. Check: entry appears in the tracker before the content ships as a paid ad.
7. **Flag expiring rights weekly.** On the weekly run, checks the tracker for anything expiring inside 30 days and flags it before a whitelisted post silently reverts to organic-only. Skill: `none`. Output: expiry flag list. Check: every flag reaches a person before the expiry date.

Outputs: creator brief, rights log entry, expiry flag list. QA: no UGC used without a concept brief; no paid use without a logged rights expiry date. Read point: first-read window once the sourced or briefed content runs as an ad, per CREA-W01. Duration: agent time: 1 to 2 hours per creator; weekly scan: 30 minutes.

### CREA-W04 Direct a product photo or video shoot

Trigger: event: shoot approved. Human role: Creative producer running a shoot to spec. Capabilities: CREA-G01, CREA-C12. Needs: approved creative or concept brief needing real photography, product facts: real dimensions, material, color, fit, brand kit.

1. **Set image-accuracy direction.** Sets direction so the product shown will match what arrives at home: real color, scale next to a known object, material texture visible. Skill: `crea/briefing/shoot-and-photo-direction`. Output: image-accuracy brief. Check: brief names the exact product variant or SKU being shot, not a generic stand-in.
2. **Build the shot list.** Turns the brief into a numbered shot list, with angles and gaze direction toward the CTA when a person is in frame. Skill: `crea/briefing/shoot-and-photo-direction`. Output: shot list. Check: every shot ties to a specific ad or page use, not a generic library.
3. **Book studio or location and model.** Books the shoot slot, studio or location, and any model, from the approved production budget line. Skill: `none`. Output: booking confirmation. Check: booking sits inside the monthly production budget from CREA-W09 or CREA-W10. Ask the founder if: the shoot needs a budget line not yet approved.
4. **Get signed releases.** Collects the signed model and location release before the shoot starts, never after. Skill: `none`. Output: signed releases on file. Check: release is on file before a single frame is used in any draft.
5. **Run the shoot against the shot list.** A person runs the shoot; CREA checks each delivered frame against the shot list and the image-accuracy brief as it comes in. Skill: `none`. Output: raw frame set checked against the shot list. Check: every shot list line has a matching delivered frame or a named gap.
6. **Retouch QA against the real product.** Checks that retouching does not alter the real product's color, fit, scale or material past what a customer will actually receive. Skill: `crea/briefing/shoot-and-photo-direction`. Output: QA'd final frame set. Check: no retouch changes a fact-bearing detail: size, color or material.
7. **File to the asset library.** Names, tags and files the final frames using the standing naming schema. Skill: `crea/production-ops/naming-and-tagging`. Output: filed, tagged asset set. Check: every frame has a stable id and a release on file before it is usable downstream.

Outputs: shot list, signed releases, QA'd final frame set. QA: release on file before use; no retouch misrepresents the real product. Read point: not applicable directly; frames feed CREA-W01's assets and are read at that workflow's read point. Duration: agent time: 2 to 4 hours of briefing and QA; waiting time: the shoot day plus retouch turnaround, 3 to 7 days.

### CREA-W05 Turn a live product page into a fast first-pass ad variant set

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Fast-turn creative drafter for a new or under-tested SKU. Capabilities: CREA-C37, CREA-C05, CREA-C26. Needs: live product page URL, claim tiers, brand voice.

1. **Read the live page.** Reads the live product page and pulls its images, price and stated claims exactly as shown. Skill: `crea/special-formats/product-page-to-ad`. Output: page facts: images, price, stated claims. Check: every claim pulled matches the page's exact wording, nothing added.
2. **Cross-check claims against the brand record.** Matches each pulled claim to the brand's claim tiers and drops anything the page states that is not on record. Skill: `none`. Output: cleared claim list. Check: no claim survives that is not in the approved or site-backed tier.
3. **Draft first-pass static variants.** Drafts 3 to 5 static ad variants pairing the real page images with headline options built from the cleared claims. Skill: `crea/make-creatives/static`. Output: 3 to 5 static ad drafts. Check: product image is the real photo from the page, not redrawn.
4. **Draft a first-pass video or UGC concept.** Drafts one short video or UGC concept using the same cleared claims, as a second format option. Skill: `crea/write-copy/scripts`. Output: one short video concept. Check: concept states the same claims as the static set, no drift.
5. **Write headline and primary-text sets.** Writes headline and primary-text variant sets for the static drafts, checked against length and image fit. Skill: `crea/write-copy/headlines`. Output: headline and primary-text set. Check: each headline fits the platform's current character limit.
6. **Run the copy pass.** Applies the core copywriting rules (reading level, single idea) to the full first-pass set. Skill: `crea/compliance/copy-craft-rules`. Output: edited first-pass set. Check: reading level under grade 10.
7. **Hand off as a fast-test candidate.** Packages the first-pass set as a candidate for CREA-W01's test-plan step, flagged as unproven and needing its own read. Skill: `none`. Output: fast-test candidate package. Check: package is clearly flagged as first-pass, not a finished concept.

Outputs: static ad drafts, video concept, headline and primary-text set. QA: no claim beyond the page's own claim-tier match; real product photo used. Read point: feeds into CREA-W01's test plan and read point once launched. Duration: agent time: 1 to 2 hours.

### CREA-W06 Localise a creative and copy set for a new market or device

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Localisation editor for a mobile-first, non-US market. Capabilities: CREA-C22, CREA-C26. Needs: source creative and copy set, target market's payment, delivery and trust facts, language pair if dual-language.

1. **Confirm the target spec.** Confirms the device target (mobile-first, vertical-only formats) and market facts (COD availability, EMI, delivery windows, local payment logos) for the target market. Skill: `crea/briefing/localisation-spec`. Output: target spec sheet. Check: every trust fact on the sheet matches the current brand policy, not an assumption. Ask the founder if: the brand has no policy on file for this market yet.
2. **Re-cut for vertical, mobile-first delivery.** Re-crops or re-composes the framing for vertical, mobile-first delivery, and flags any asset that cannot be honestly re-cropped. Skill: `crea/derivatives/resize-for-placement`. Output: vertical-first asset set. Check: no important element is cropped out of frame.
3. **Localise the copy.** Rewrites hooks and copy for the local reading level and, where needed, drafts a dual-language hook pairing that matches how the persona actually speaks. Skill: `crea/write-copy/hooks`. Output: localised copy set. Check: dual-language line reads as a natural code-switch, not a literal translation.
4. **Swap in local trust facts.** Replaces any non-local trust line (a foreign currency guarantee, a foreign shipping time) with the real local fact: COD availability, local return window, local payment logos. Skill: `crea/pricing-and-naming/price-framing`. Output: trust-line-corrected copy. Check: every trust fact traces to the current local policy record.
5. **Run the compliance pass.** Runs the claim and copywriting-rules check on the localised set. Skill: `crea/compliance/copy-craft-rules`. Output: audited localised set. Check: no claim carried over that does not hold in the local market.
6. **Hand off as a market-tagged variant.** Tags the finished set with the market and device so it never runs against the wrong audience. Skill: `crea/production-ops/naming-and-tagging`. Output: tagged localised asset set. Check: every asset carries a market tag before handoff.

Outputs: vertical-first asset set, localised copy set, tagged localised asset set. QA: no non-local trust claim survives; dual-language line reads natural. Read point: same read point as the parent test once launched in-market. Duration: agent time: 2 to 3 hours per market.

### CREA-W07 Diagnose creative fatigue and brief the revival

Trigger: weekly. Human role: Creative performance analyst watching for wear-out. Capabilities: CREA-C15. Needs: lifecycle metrics: days to fatigue, frequency, ad-level results, hook bank for the fatigued ad's angle.

1. **Pull lifecycle metrics.** Pulls each live ad's frequency, hold rate and CPA trend against the account's own historical fatigue curve. Skill: `none`. Output: fatigue candidate list. Check: every candidate compares to this account's own curve, not a generic industry number.
2. **Rule out a small-audience-pool cause.** Checks delivery volume and frequency to see if the drop is really audience exhaustion, not creative wear-out. Skill: `crea/fatigue-and-teardown/fatigue-diagnosis`. Output: cause split: audience-pool versus creative. Check: verdict names the specific number that decided it.
3. **Diagnose the creative cause.** For creative-caused fatigue, identifies which part wore out: hook, visual, or the whole concept. Skill: `crea/fatigue-and-teardown/fatigue-diagnosis`. Output: fatigue diagnosis note. Check: diagnosis names one specific part, not a general 'the ad is tired'.
4. **Prescribe the cheapest real fix first.** Proposes a hook swap or format rotation before a whole new concept, in that order, using the existing hook bank. Skill: `crea/write-copy/hooks`. Output: revival brief. Check: brief tries the cheapest fix first unless the diagnosis rules it out.
5. **Route audience-pool cases to MEDIA.** For audience-pool causes, hands the finding to MEDIA instead of commissioning new creative. Skill: `none`. Output: handoff note to MEDIA. Check: note is explicit that this is not a creative problem.
6. **Log the fatigue event.** Writes the registry entry for the fatigue event and its prescribed fix, feeding CREA-W08's teardown history. Skill: `shared/registry-entry`. Output: registry entry. Check: entry links the original ad id to the fix tried.

Outputs: fatigue diagnosis note, revival brief, registry entry. QA: diagnosis names a specific metric and comparison, not a guess. Read point: weekly, against the account's rolling fatigue curve. Duration: agent time: about 1 hour per week across the live set.

### CREA-W08 Tear down the week's winners and losers

Trigger: weekly. Human role: Creative analyst writing the weekly breakdown. Capabilities: CREA-C16. Needs: ad-level metrics for the week, account averages, ad creative (image or video) and copy, competitor data, signal only.

1. **Pull the week's ad-level results.** Pulls every ad's metrics next to the account average for the same period. Skill: `none`. Output: ranked ad list for the week. Check: totals and per-ad breakdowns are kept separate, never summed into one number.
2. **Break each ad into its parts.** For the top and bottom ads, breaks the creative into hook, structure, proof, offer and CTA. Skill: `crea/fatigue-and-teardown/winner-teardown`. Output: part-by-part breakdown per ad. Check: every part is named, none skipped.
3. **Write the hypothesis per ad.** Writes a specific hypothesis for why each ad worked or did not, backed by the number that shows it. Skill: `crea/fatigue-and-teardown/winner-teardown`. Output: hypothesis per ad. Check: hypothesis cites a number, not an opinion.
4. **Check against competitor signal.** Notes whether a winning structure echoes a competitor pattern already tagged as signal, without claiming causation. Skill: `crea/research/competitor-signal-pull`. Output: competitor-signal note. Check: note is marked signal only, never proof.
5. **Assign a funnel role.** From the real numbers, assigns each ad a funnel role: cold hook winner, retention proof, or bottom-funnel closer. Skill: `none`. Output: funnel-role tags. Check: tag matches the metric that drove it, for example high CTR with low CVR means top-funnel only.
6. **Write the breakdown report.** Assembles the week's breakdown into a short report for the founder. Skill: `shared/report-section`. Output: weekly breakdown report. Check: report is under the founder's usual reading time; digits not spelled out.
7. **Log the hypothesis.** Writes each hypothesis to the registry so future angle and hook work in CREA-W01 starts from it. Skill: `shared/registry-entry`. Output: registry entries. Check: each entry links to the ad id and the metric window used.

Outputs: breakdown report, hypothesis log entries. QA: verdict backed by a specific number; competitor material marked signal only. Read point: weekly, on the last full day's data. Duration: agent time: 1 to 2 hours.

### CREA-W09 Plan the monthly creative production pipeline and portfolio mix

Trigger: monthly. Human role: Creative producer planning next month's slate. Capabilities: CREA-C18. Needs: account's own hit-rate history, current portfolio mix by angle, format and persona, monthly budget line.

1. **Pull the account's hit rate.** Pulls the real ratio of winning to tested creatives over the last quarter, by format and angle. Skill: `none`. Output: hit-rate table by format and angle. Check: rate is computed in code from real results, not estimated.
2. **Audit portfolio coverage.** Checks which persona, angle and format combinations are under-tested or over-concentrated. Skill: `shared/surface-audit`. Output: coverage gap list. Check: every active persona has at least one live test in the last 60 days, or is flagged.
3. **Size next month's volume.** Sizes how many new concepts, variants and formats to produce next month from the hit rate and the budget line, not a fixed quota. Skill: `crea/production-ops/pipeline-planning`. Output: monthly production plan. Check: plan's volume is derived from the hit rate and budget, shown as a calculation.
4. **Sequence the pipeline stages.** Lays out the month across research, concept, build, variations, feedback and launch stages with dates. Skill: `crea/production-ops/pipeline-planning`. Output: sequenced pipeline calendar. Check: every stage has a date and an owner.
5. **Flag budget risk early.** Cross-checks the planned volume against the cost-per-asset trend from CREA-W10 to flag a likely overrun before it happens. Skill: `crea/production-ops/cost-tracking`. Output: budget risk flag, if any. Check: flag is raised before the month starts, not after an overrun.
6. **Publish the plan.** Writes the plan up for the founder and MEDIA to see. Skill: `shared/report-section`. Output: monthly plan document. Check: plan is readable in one pass, digits not spelled out.

Outputs: monthly production plan, sequenced pipeline calendar, budget risk flag. QA: volume derived from hit rate and budget, not a guess. Read point: monthly, first business day of the month, on the prior full month's data. Duration: agent time: about 2 hours.

### CREA-W10 Track production cost per asset against the monthly budget

Trigger: weekly. Human role: Creative budget tracker. Capabilities: CREA-C54. Needs: real cost per asset produced: AI credits, freelancer or agency invoices, stock license fees, monthly budget line from CREA-W09.

1. **Log real costs.** Logs every real cost as it is incurred, generation credits, invoices, license fees, against the specific asset it produced. Skill: `crea/production-ops/cost-tracking`. Output: cost log entries. Check: every cost ties to a named asset id, never a lump sum.
2. **Compute cost per asset.** Computes cost per finished asset by format, in code. Skill: `crea/production-ops/cost-tracking`. Output: cost-per-asset table by format. Check: computation is shown, not estimated.
3. **Compare to the monthly plan.** Checks running spend against the monthly budget line from CREA-W09. Skill: `crea/production-ops/cost-tracking`. Output: spend-versus-plan status. Check: status shows unavailable, not zero, for any cost not yet logged.
4. **Flag a likely overrun.** If the trend line crosses the budget before month end, flags it to the founder with the number and the date it will cross. Skill: `none`. Output: overrun flag. Check: flag names the exact projected crossing date. Ask the founder if: the projected overrun exceeds 15% of the monthly line.
5. **Log to the registry.** Writes the weekly cost snapshot to the registry for the next monthly plan. Skill: `shared/registry-entry`. Output: registry entry. Check: entry is dated and links to the current monthly plan.

Outputs: cost-per-asset table, spend-versus-plan status, overrun flag. QA: every cost tied to a named asset; missing cost shown as unavailable, never zero. Read point: weekly, on real invoices and credit usage logged that week. Duration: agent time: 30 to 45 minutes.

### CREA-W11 Maintain the naming, tagging and platform-spec standard

Trigger: monthly. Human role: Creative operations owner of the naming standard. Capabilities: CREA-C20. Needs: current naming and tagging schema, each ad platform's current character and spec limits.

1. **Audit the current schema against real assets.** Checks a sample of recent assets against the standing naming and tagging schema for drift. Skill: `crea/production-ops/naming-and-tagging`. Output: drift report. Check: every drift instance is named, not just counted.
2. **Refresh the platform spec sheet.** Re-checks each ad platform's current character limits, aspect ratios and file-size caps, since these change without notice. Skill: `crea/production-ops/naming-and-tagging`. Output: refreshed platform-spec sheet. Check: sheet's last-checked date is under 30 days when copy is being written to a limit.
3. **Update the schema if needed.** Adjusts the schema for a new format, persona or offer type added since the last check. Skill: `crea/production-ops/naming-and-tagging`. Output: updated schema. Check: update does not break the id stability of existing assets.
4. **Re-tag drifted assets.** Fixes tags on any asset found off-schema in the audit. Skill: `crea/production-ops/naming-and-tagging`. Output: re-tagged assets. Check: every drifted asset now matches the current schema.
5. **Publish the standard.** Writes the current schema and spec sheet to a place every CREA workflow reads from. Skill: `none`. Output: published standard. Check: publish date is visible to every workflow that reads it.

Outputs: refreshed platform-spec sheet, updated naming and tagging schema. QA: spec sheet checked within 30 days when in active use. Read point: monthly. Duration: agent time: about 1 hour.

### CREA-W12 Set and update where AI may be used in the pipeline

Trigger: continuous. Human role: AI-use governance owner for the creative pipeline. Capabilities: CREA-C21. Needs: current AI-use policy, any new AI tool or capability entering the pipeline.

1. **List where AI touches the pipeline.** Lists every point AI drafts, generates or grades something across CREA's workflows. Skill: `crea/compliance/ai-use-governance`. Output: AI touchpoint map. Check: map covers every workflow, none skipped.
2. **Classify each touchpoint.** Classifies each touchpoint as drafting or grunt work, allowed unsupervised, or final ideation and authorship, never unsupervised. Skill: `crea/compliance/ai-use-governance`. Output: classified touchpoint list. Check: no final-ideation touchpoint is marked unsupervised.
3. **Ground each AI draft in brand facts.** Confirms each drafting touchpoint is fed the brand's own facts (claim tiers, voice, product data), not generic training knowledge. Skill: `crea/compliance/ai-use-governance`. Output: grounding check result. Check: every drafting step names its brand-fact input.
4. **Update the policy for a new tool.** When a new AI capability enters the pipeline, for example an AI-actor or voice-clone tool, classifies it and adds it to the policy before use. Skill: `crea/compliance/ai-use-governance`. Output: updated AI-use policy. Check: policy update happens before the tool's first real use. Ask the founder if: a new tool would touch final ideation or unsupervised authorship.
5. **Publish and log the policy.** Publishes the current policy and logs the update. Skill: `shared/registry-entry`. Output: published policy and registry entry. Check: policy is dated and visible to every CREA workflow.

Outputs: AI touchpoint map, updated AI-use policy. QA: no final-ideation step runs unsupervised; every AI draft grounded in brand facts. Read point: continuous, reviewed at every new tool or quarterly, whichever comes first. Duration: agent time: ongoing, about 30 minutes per review.

### CREA-W13 Frame a price or offer's value in copy

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Pricing and offer copywriter. Capabilities: CREA-C31. Needs: brand pricing and policies, the specific price or offer to frame, positioning.

1. **Pull the real price and policy facts.** Pulls the exact price, any discount, and the policy limits ops can honor, from the brand brain. Skill: `crea/pricing-and-naming/price-framing`. Output: price and policy fact sheet. Check: every number matches the current live price, not a cached one.
2. **Choose the ending style.** Picks a charm ending (.99/.95) or a round-number ending based on the brand's positioning: value versus premium. Skill: `crea/pricing-and-naming/price-framing`. Output: ending-style decision. Check: decision matches the stated positioning, not a default.
3. **Build the cost-per-day reframe if it helps.** Builds a cost-per-day or cost-per-use reframe when it makes the price feel smaller honestly. Skill: `crea/pricing-and-naming/price-framing`. Output: reframe line, if used. Check: the math behind the reframe is shown and correct.
4. **State the honest reason for a low price.** If the price looks unusually low, writes the real reason, for example clearance, factory-direct, or a festive sale, rather than leaving it unexplained. Skill: `crea/pricing-and-naming/price-framing`. Output: reason line. Check: reason is a real, checkable fact, not a manufactured urgency.
5. **Check the guarantee wording.** Matches any guarantee or urgency line exactly to what ops can honor. Skill: `crea/pricing-and-naming/price-framing`. Output: checked guarantee or urgency line. Check: wording matches ops eligibility exactly, no rounding up. Ask the founder if: the requested wording exceeds what ops confirmed it can honor.
6. **Run the claim pass.** Runs the claim-tier check on the finished price copy block. Skill: `none`. Output: audited price copy block. Check: no claim beyond the approved or site-backed tier.

Outputs: price copy block, price-framing line. QA: guarantee wording matches ops exactly; no fabricated deadline. Read point: read at whichever ad or page workflow requested it. Duration: agent time: 30 to 45 minutes.

### CREA-W14 Name a new product or proprietary system

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Naming and positioning writer. Capabilities: CREA-C32. Needs: product or system facts, the benefit or story it should carry, any real named person attached.

1. **Pull the benefit to carry.** Pulls the single strongest benefit or mechanism the name should carry, from the product facts and the angle brief if one exists. Skill: `crea/pricing-and-naming/product-naming`. Output: core benefit statement. Check: benefit is one sentence, one idea.
2. **Draft name candidates.** Drafts 8 to 15 name candidates that carry the benefit or attach to a real named person. Skill: `crea/pricing-and-naming/product-naming`. Output: name candidate list. Check: every candidate ties to the benefit statement, not a generic-sounding word.
3. **Check for real-person attachment.** If a real named person, for example the founder or a formulator, is attached, confirms the story is real and permissioned. Skill: `crea/pricing-and-naming/product-naming`. Output: attachment check result. Check: no invented person or credential. Ask the founder if: a candidate implies a credential not on file.
4. **Screen for conflicts.** Screens candidates for an obvious trademark or marketplace-listing conflict via public search. Skill: `crea/pricing-and-naming/product-naming`. Output: conflict-screened shortlist. Check: shortlist notes any close public match found.
5. **Rank and present to the founder.** Ranks the shortlist against the benefit fit and presents it as a founder_gate decision. Skill: `shared/rank-candidates`. Output: ranked name shortlist. Check: ranking states the reason for the order. Ask the founder if: always, naming is founder_gate.

Outputs: name candidate list, ranked name shortlist. QA: no invented credential or story. Read point: not applicable; the decision happens at the founder gate. Duration: agent time: about 1 hour.

### CREA-W15 Refresh objection-handling and retargeting continuity copy

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Bottom-funnel copywriter. Capabilities: CREA-C24. Needs: current objection map, customer data source: support tickets, reviews, return reasons, existing retargeting sequence.

1. **Pull real objections from customer data.** Pulls the most common real objections from support tickets, reviews and return reasons, ranked by frequency. Skill: `crea/special-formats/objection-retargeting-copy`. Output: ranked objection list with quoted sources. Check: every objection has a real quoted source and a count. Ask the founder if: no support or returns data source is connected.
2. **Write one line per objection.** Writes one bottom-funnel ad line or copy block per objection, prefacing the most common one early rather than saving it for an FAQ. Skill: `crea/special-formats/objection-retargeting-copy`. Output: objection-handling copy set. Check: the top-ranked objection is addressed in the first line, not buried.
3. **Keep the sequence consistent.** Checks the new lines against the existing retargeting sequence so the same objection is not answered twice with contradicting facts. Skill: `crea/special-formats/objection-retargeting-copy`. Output: sequence-checked copy set. Check: no two steps in the sequence contradict each other.
4. **Run the claim pass.** Runs the claim-tier check on every objection response, since these often brush against guarantee or comparison claims. Skill: `none`. Output: audited copy set. Check: no forbidden-tier claim used to defuse an objection.
5. **Hand off to the owning surface.** Hands the finished lines to whichever surface will run them: an ad via CREA-W01, or an email or SMS block via Lifecycle. Skill: `none`. Output: handoff package. Check: handoff names the exact surface and owner.

Outputs: ranked objection list, objection-handling copy set. QA: every objection traced to a real quoted source; top objection addressed first, not last. Read point: at the read point of whichever surface deploys the copy. Duration: agent time: 1 to 2 hours.

### CREA-W16 Write COD-to-prepaid and RTO-reduction creative messaging

Trigger: on demand. Human role: COD-conversion and delivery-trust copywriter. Capabilities: CREA-C44. Needs: current COD and prepaid mix and RTO rate, prepaid incentive if any, delivery-trust facts: courier, timelines, ID verification.

1. **Pull the real RTO and COD numbers.** Pulls the account's current COD share and RTO rate to size how much this messaging matters right now. Skill: `none`. Output: RTO and COD baseline. Check: numbers pulled from the metric contract, not estimated.
2. **Write the prepaid nudge.** Writes the ad and post-purchase line that pushes toward prepaid, discount, faster dispatch, ID-verified courier trust, honest and specific. Skill: `crea/special-formats/cod-prepaid-rto-messaging`. Output: prepaid nudge copy. Check: any discount or speed claim matches what ops actually offers.
3. **Write the delivery-trust messaging.** Writes the trust lines that lower RTO risk: real courier name, real delivery window, call-before-delivery fact if true. Skill: `crea/special-formats/cod-prepaid-rto-messaging`. Output: delivery-trust copy block. Check: every fact matches current ops policy, not an aspiration.
4. **Run the claim pass.** Runs the claim-tier check, since delivery-time and discount claims are a common source of drift. Skill: `none`. Output: audited COD and RTO copy. Check: no delivery-time claim beyond the current real average.
5. **Hand off with the baseline attached.** Hands the copy to MEDIA or the Site specialist with the RTO and COD baseline attached so impact can be measured later. Skill: `shared/registry-entry`. Output: handoff package with baseline. Check: baseline numbers travel with the copy.

Outputs: prepaid nudge copy, delivery-trust copy block. QA: every delivery or discount fact matches current ops policy. Read point: next full month's COD share and RTO rate after deployment. Duration: agent time: about 1 hour.

### CREA-W17 Build Amazon A+ Content and marketplace brand-store modules

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Marketplace creative module designer. Capabilities: CREA-C45. Needs: marketplace's current module template grid: Amazon A+, Flipkart or Myntra brand store, product facts and real photos, claim tiers.

1. **Pull the current template grid.** Pulls the marketplace's own current A+ Content or brand-store module system, since these are fixed and change per platform update. Skill: `crea/special-formats/marketplace-modules`. Output: current module grid spec. Check: spec checked within the last 30 days.
2. **Pick the modules for this product.** Picks the banner, infographic and comparison-chart modules that fit this product's real differentiators. Skill: `crea/special-formats/marketplace-modules`. Output: module selection. Check: every module chosen has content to fill it, not a placeholder.
3. **Write the module copy.** Writes the copy for each module within its exact character and layout limits. Skill: `crea/write-copy/primary-text`. Output: module copy set. Check: copy fits each module's real space, no overflow.
4. **Direct the module imagery.** Directs the real product photo or diagram used in each visual module, no product redraw where fit or scale is the point. Skill: `crea/briefing/shoot-and-photo-direction`. Output: module imagery direction. Check: real product photo used for every fit or scale claim.
5. **Run the claim pass.** Runs the claim-tier check on every comparison-chart line especially, since these draw the most marketplace policy scrutiny. Skill: `none`. Output: audited module set. Check: no comparison claim without evidence on file.
6. **Hand off to Product and Supply.** Hands the finished modules to the Product and Supply specialist, who owns the actual marketplace listing surface. Skill: `none`. Output: handoff package. Check: package includes the exact module grid slot for each piece.

Outputs: module selection, module copy set, module imagery direction. QA: module grid spec checked within 30 days; real photo used for fit or scale claims. Read point: next full month's listing conversion rate after publish, read by Product and Supply. Duration: agent time: 2 to 3 hours.

### CREA-W18 Build and maintain an AI character or mascot sheet

Trigger: on demand. Human role: Character and mascot continuity owner. Capabilities: CREA-C47. Needs: approved character concept, brand kit and voice.

1. **Define the character's fixed traits.** Fixes the character's core traits so the identity does not drift across generations. Skill: `crea/special-formats/mascot-sheet`. Output: character trait sheet. Check: every trait is specific enough to reject a wrong regeneration.
2. **Generate the reference sheet.** Generates the fixed set of expressions, angles and outfit views used as the anchor for every future generation. Skill: `crea/special-formats/mascot-sheet`. Output: reference sheet: expressions, angles, outfit. Check: sheet covers the angles and expressions the pipeline actually reuses.
3. **File and version the sheet.** Names, tags and versions the reference sheet so every later generation cites the exact version used. Skill: `crea/production-ops/naming-and-tagging`. Output: versioned, filed reference sheet. Check: version id is stable and referenced by every downstream asset.
4. **Check each new use against the sheet.** Before any new asset uses the character, checks it against the current reference sheet for identity drift. Skill: `crea/special-formats/mascot-sheet`. Output: drift check result. Check: any drift is caught before the asset ships.
5. **Update the sheet on a real change.** When the founder approves a real change, for example a new outfit or expression, updates the sheet as a new version rather than editing history. Skill: `crea/special-formats/mascot-sheet`. Output: new sheet version. Check: old version stays on file, not overwritten.

Outputs: reference sheet, versioned character asset. QA: no identity drift ships uncaught. Read point: not applicable directly; feeds whichever creative uses the character. Duration: agent time: 1 to 2 hours to build, 15 minutes per reuse check.

### CREA-W19 Generate a synthetic AI-actor UGC video ad

Trigger: on demand, founder_gate. Human role: Fast-test video producer, no shoot. Capabilities: CREA-C36. Needs: written script, founder approval to use a synthetic actor for this test, claim tiers.

1. **Confirm the founder gate.** Confirms the founder has approved using a licensed AI actor for this specific test before generating anything. Skill: `none`. Output: gate confirmation. Check: approval is on record for this specific use, not a standing blanket approval. Ask the founder if: no approval is on file yet.
2. **Finalize the script for a synthetic read.** Finalizes the hook-to-camera script, since a synthetic actor reads exactly what is written with no ad lib. Skill: `crea/write-copy/scripts`. Output: read-ready script. Check: script contains no line that needs natural ad lib to land.
3. **Run the claim pass.** Runs the claim-tier check before generation, since a synthetic ad cannot be caught and reshot the way a human one can. Skill: `none`. Output: audited script. Check: no unverified claim reaches generation.
4. **Generate the AI-actor video.** Generates the finished vertical video using the licensed AI actor reading the audited script. Skill: `crea/special-formats/ai-actor-ugc`. Output: draft AI-actor video. Check: actor's on-screen claims match the audited script exactly, no drift from generation.
5. **Caption and QA.** Adds burned-in captions and checks the finished video against the QA checklist before it counts as ready. Skill: `crea/derivatives/caption-and-alt-text`. Output: captioned, QA'd video. Check: captions match the spoken line exactly.
6. **Flag it as synthetic in the handoff.** Flags the asset as AI-actor generated in its metadata and handoff note, for platform disclosure rules and internal tracking. Skill: `crea/production-ops/naming-and-tagging`. Output: tagged, disclosed asset. Check: disclosure tag is present before handoff to MEDIA.

Outputs: draft AI-actor video, captioned QA'd video. QA: founder approval on file before generation; no unverified claim in the read. Read point: same read point as CREA-W01 once launched as a test. Duration: agent time: about 1 hour plus generation wait, typically 10 to 30 minutes.

### CREA-W20 Clone a consenting voice for voiceover variants

Trigger: on demand, founder_gate, only after consent is on file. Human role: Voiceover variant producer. Capabilities: CREA-C46. Needs: documented written consent from the founder or contracted creator, script variants needing voiceover.

1. **Confirm written consent is on file.** Confirms the specific person's documented written consent is on file for voice cloning before doing anything else. Skill: `none`. Output: consent confirmation. Check: consent document is dated, named and specific to this use. Ask the founder if: consent is missing, expired, or does not cover this specific use.
2. **Prepare the voice model.** Prepares the cloned voice model from the consented source recording. Skill: `crea/special-formats/voice-clone-variants`. Output: voice model ready for use. Check: model is built only from the consented recording.
3. **Generate the script variant reads.** Generates voiceover for each approved script variant using the cloned voice. Skill: `crea/special-formats/voice-clone-variants`. Output: voiceover variant set. Check: each variant reads the exact audited script line.
4. **QA against the real person's cadence.** Checks the generated read against the real person's known cadence and pronunciation for anything that would sound obviously wrong. Skill: `crea/special-formats/voice-clone-variants`. Output: QA'd voiceover set. Check: no line mispronounces a name or term the real person would say correctly.
5. **Tag and log the use.** Tags every generated line with the consent record id and logs the use so consent scope is never exceeded. Skill: `shared/registry-entry`. Output: logged, tagged voiceover set. Check: log entry shows total uses against any use-count limit in the consent.

Outputs: voiceover variant set, registry log entry. QA: consent on file before any generation; use logged against any consent limit. Read point: same read point as the ad workflow that uses the voiceover. Duration: agent time: about 1 hour plus generation wait.

### CREA-W21 Score a creative batch before it enters paid testing

Trigger: event: before a new batch enters paid testing. Human role: Pre-flight creative QA scorer. Capabilities: CREA-C42. Needs: finished draft batch, the account's own past predictive-score-to-outcome record, if any.

1. **Run the predictive pass.** Runs each draft through the predictive scoring pass, flagging weak elements. Skill: `crea/production-ops/predictive-scoring`. Output: predicted score per draft, per element. Check: score names the specific element flagged, hook clarity, headline-image mismatch or CTA visibility, not just an overall number.
2. **Compare to this account's own history.** Checks whether this account's past predictive scores actually tracked real outcomes, and downweights the score if not. Skill: `crea/production-ops/predictive-scoring`. Output: calibrated score. Check: calibration is shown, not assumed accurate.
3. **Flag weak elements for a fix.** Sends any draft with a flagged weak element back for a specific fix, not a general redo. Skill: `crea/production-ops/predictive-scoring`. Output: fix list per draft. Check: fix list names the exact element, not 'make it better'.
4. **Re-score after fix.** Re-runs the pass on fixed drafts before clearing them for test. Skill: `crea/production-ops/predictive-scoring`. Output: post-fix score. Check: post-fix score is logged next to the original for calibration tracking.
5. **Clear the batch.** Clears the batch for CREA-W01's launch checklist once every draft passes or has a named, accepted risk. Skill: `none`. Output: cleared batch. Check: every draft is either passed or has a named, founder-accepted risk. Ask the founder if: a draft fails but the founder wants to test it anyway.

Outputs: predicted score per draft, fix list, cleared batch. QA: score calibrated against this account's real outcomes, not assumed. Read point: the batch's actual paid-test read point, used to recalibrate the scorer over time. Duration: agent time: 30 to 45 minutes per batch.

### CREA-W22 Resize, caption and cut one master creative into every derivative

Trigger: event: master creative approved. Human role: Creative post-production operator. Capabilities: CREA-C39, CREA-C40, CREA-C41. Needs: one approved master creative, the platform placement list it needs to cover.
